# Verification against the record (ROADMAP 4.5)

561 claims checked against Tier 1 records and Tier 2 reporting (POLICY 11): 39 contradicted-by-record, 522 corroborated. 2 of them keep `revised-by-host` because the host later revised them. ## contradicted-by-record (39)

### C-164 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the probable cause statement overview, the host says the indictment charges Robinson with a count six because children were present at the time of the shooting.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 4) lists Count 6 as telling the roommate not to talk to police (witness tampering, as read at S-7 05:01:11), with 'children were present' a separate allegation, so Count 6 is not a children-present count.
  - S-1 p. 4 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "(Count 5) and not to talk to police (Count 6). Children were present at the time of the shooting"
  - S-7 (05:01:11) (charging_allegation, defense attorney Mr. Novak): ""to wit directing his roommate to stay silent if police questioned the roommate.""

### C-511 (ep 6)
- **Claim:** The host says most of the people with credibility are ballistics experts who say the wound seen at the front is an exit wound, while she says she cannot judge it herself because she is not an expert.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7 08:16:02) finds the bullet traveled front to back and did not exit Kirk's body, so the front wound was not an exit wound.
  - S-7 (08:16:02) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "the bullet traveled anatomically from front to back and downward after striking Mr. Kirk. The projectile did not exit his body."

### C-752 (ep 9)
- **Claim:** The host says Tyler Robinson himself has not confessed to anything.
- **Record:** In his recorded statement (S-5 Exhibit 16, 02:26:16), Twiggs says Robinson confirmed in person that what he had said was true and that he wished he hadn't done it, and Agent Hull testified (S-3 02:23:13) that Robinson told his immediate family he had been involved in the shooting.
  - S-5 (02:26:16) (exhibit, Lance Twiggs, Robinson's roommate/partner (recorded interview, State's Exhibit 16)): "I just asked him in person if what he said was true the night before and he said it was."
  - S-5 (02:26:16) (exhibit, Lance Twiggs, Robinson's roommate/partner (recorded interview, State's Exhibit 16)): "He started crying a little bit and said he wishes he hadn't done it"
  - S-3 (02:23:13) (testimony, Agent Hull, former Utah State Bureau of Investigation agent (witness; Rev also renders Holt/Hall)): "He had informed, I believe, his immediate family that he had been involved in the shooting and that he wanted to turn himself in."

### C-907 (ep 10)
- **Claim:** In the clip, Range Day Bro says a video made by a Marine friend shows the angle the shot came from and the circled area of Charlie's neck, with an exit wound following a split second after as from an entry and exit wound.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7 07:46:29) finds the bullet did not exit Kirk's body but came to rest in the tissues of his upper back, so there was no exit wound.
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The bullet did not exit Mr. Kirk's body, but came to rest and tissues in his upper back left of the middle line."

### C-965 (ep 11)
- **Claim:** The host says that, according to her source, Tyler Robinson has never even stepped foot on the UVU campus.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified that UVU surveillance video shows Robinson on campus about four times that day, including returning on foot just after 10:00 AM (S-2 06:35:55; S-3 00:24:01, 00:48:03).
  - S-2 (06:35:55) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "That is correct, yes."
  - S-3 (00:24:01) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "It does, yes."
  - S-3 (00:48:03) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "Just after 10:00 AM."

### C-1459 (ep 16)
- **Claim:** Former Navy SEAL Robert J. O'Neill, on the Piers Morgan Show, stated the wound visible in the Charlie Kirk video appeared to be an exit wound rather than an entry wound.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7 07:46:29, 08:16:02) finds the bullet travelled front to back and did not exit Kirk's body but came to rest in his upper back, so the visible wound was not an exit wound.
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The bullet did not exit Mr. Kirk's body, but came to rest and tissues in his upper back left of the middle line."
  - S-7 (08:16:02) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "the bullet traveled anatomically from front to back and downward after striking Mr. Kirk. The projectile did not exit his body."

### C-1617 (ep 18)
- **Claim:** The host says Charlie Kirk's death certificate does not reflect a recovered .30-06 bullet, indicating that caliber was not recovered from his body.
- **Record:** The ruling (S-7 07:46:29) finds the bullet fragments recovered from Kirk's body were a .30 caliber class round, and the lab result read in court calls the jacket fragment consistent with a .30-06 (S-7 exhibit), contrary to the inference that this caliber was not recovered.
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "Mr. Kirk died by a gunshot wound to his neck. Forensic examination of bullet fragments found in Mr. Kirk's body revealed the bullet to be a 30 caliber class round."
  - S-5 (05:30:42) (testimony, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "Exhibit 6 is the bullet jacket fragment, as well as some other fragments that were recovered from Mr. Kirk at the time of autopsy."
  - S-7 (03:52:34) (exhibit, prosecutor Ryan McBride): "the recovered jacket fragment from autopsy consists of a 30 caliber class round consistent with having been fired with a .30-06."

### C-1888 (ep 20)
- **Claim:** The host says there is no footage or other indication of the drive to, or the arrival at, Timpanogos Hospital.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report records Orem Fire Station Three passing the TPUSA SUV carrying Kirk to Timpanogos Hospital and radioing it in (S-12 p. 22), and a Station Two fire captain assisting with CPR at the hospital (p. 52); Agent Hull testified that Kirk was pronounced dead there (S-2 05:59:17).
  - S-12 p. 22 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "Station Three fire personnel passed the SUV being used by the TPUSA security team to transport the victim to Timpanogos Hospital and relayed that information by radio."
  - S-12 p. 52 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Station Two then rerouted to Timpanogos Hospital, where the Fire Captain from Station Two assisted in providing CPR to Charlie Kirk at the hospital."
  - S-2 (05:59:17) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "He was, yes."

### C-1971 (ep 21)
- **Claim:** The host says Charlie Kirk was not administered any first aid, that she has close-up videos of this, and that no gauze was applied and the wound was not secured as they carried him to the hospital.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified that Kirk's security team removed him from the scene 'whilst administering first aid' (S-2 05:58:03).
  - S-2 (05:58:03) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "Mr. Kirk's detail, his own security team removed him from the scene whilst administering first aid and they conducted what would be called a hasty transport to Timpanogos Hospital."

### C-2004 (ep 22)
- **Claim:** The host says that, as is now known, Tyler Robinson was not on the Woodbury building or on campus at all, so she does not treat Casey Baugh as a suspect for providing access.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified that UVU surveillance video shows Robinson on campus about four times on September 10, including returning on foot just after 10:00 AM (S-2 06:35:55; S-3 00:24:01, 00:48:03).
  - S-2 (06:35:55) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "That is correct, yes."
  - S-3 (00:24:01) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "It does, yes."
  - S-3 (00:48:03) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "Just after 10:00 AM."

### C-2066 (ep 23)
- **Claim:** The host says she is confident Charlie Kirk was taken out by something much bigger than Turning Point USA and much bigger than Tyler Robinson, who she says she does not think even set foot on the campus that day.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified that surveillance video shows Robinson returning to campus on foot just after 10:00 AM on September 10 (S-3 00:48:03, 00:51:02).
  - S-3 (00:48:03) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "Just after 10:00 AM."
  - S-3 (00:51:02) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "This is a video of Mr. Robinson returning back to campus the same way that he did previously on foot."

### C-2281 (ep 25)
- **Claim:** The host says that on July 18 Turning Point USA received a request from UVU for Charlie to hold an event, that TPUSA instantly accepted it and made it his first stop, and she credits Sam Parker with the find.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report shows the request started with a July 10 email from TPUSA to UVU, sent by the UVU TPUSA club president at a TPUSA field representative's request, and that the club submitted the July 18 formal request to UVU (S-12 pp. 18, 41, 48), not a request from UVU to TPUSA.
  - S-12 p. 41 (exhibit, TPUSA UVU Club President (email to UVU Club Services, July 10, 2025)): "My field representative for Turning Point USA has asked me to reach out to the UVU Club Department to ask about availability on hosting an event on the Courtyard on September 10 from 9am-4pm"
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "the initial e-mail request from TPUSA to UVU on July 10, 2025, also specifically requested that the event be held at the Fountain Courtyard."
  - S-12 p. 18 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "July 18 Formal event request submitted by UVU TPUSA Club. Event described as an appearance by Charlie Kirk to discuss current events. External ticketing by TPUSA anticipated."

### C-2364 (ep 26)
- **Claim:** The host says Charlie Kirk was never rendered any first aid immediately, that the security team did not put gauze or a hand on the wound, and that his neck was not held when they carried him to the car.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified that Kirk's security team removed him from the scene 'whilst administering first aid' (S-2 05:58:03).
  - S-2 (05:58:03) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "Mr. Kirk's detail, his own security team removed him from the scene whilst administering first aid and they conducted what would be called a hasty transport to Timpanogos Hospital."

### C-2561 (ep 29)
- **Claim:** The host says that after people found it strange that Charlie Kirk was taken to Timpanogos, she and others started hearing from other people that he was definitely at UV Hospital.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified Kirk was taken to, treated at and pronounced dead at Timpanogos Regional (S-2 05:59:04-05:59:17), and the After-Action Report places CPR at Timpanogos Hospital (S-12 p. 52).
  - S-2 (05:59:04) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "I believe it's referred to as Timpanogos Regional."
  - S-2 (05:59:17) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "He was, yes."
  - S-12 p. 52 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Station Two then rerouted to Timpanogos Hospital, where the Fire Captain from Station Two assisted in providing CPR to Charlie Kirk at the hospital."

### C-3218 (ep 36)
- **Claim:** The host says she was told that the only thing Dan Flood apparently cared about was making sure the SUVs had clear in-and-out access behind Charlie, rather than other rooftops or walkways.
- **Record:** Flood's own September 8 texts to Chief Long asked for access control or a TPUSA guy on the student-accessible area above the speaker (S-12 p. 43), and the report finds he initiated that rooftop/walkway exchange (S-12 p. 19).
  - S-12 p. 43 (exhibit, Dan Flood, TPUSA Security Supervisor (text to Chief Long, Sept 8, 2025)): "I was told students have access above us, if this is true, it would be nice to either have it controlled access or allow one of my guys to be there as well."
  - S-12 p. 43 (exhibit, Chief Long and Dan Flood (text exchange, Sept 8, 2025)): "Chief Long: You want access to the roof?"
  - S-12 p. 19 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "Dan Flood initiated a text exchange with Chief Long regarding an accessible rooftop/outdoor pedestrian walkway above the canopy area where Charlie Kirk would be speaking and requested access controls."

### C-3524 (ep 40)
- **Claim:** The host says Charlie's security did not issue him first aid when he fell, nobody pulled out gauze to stop the bleeding, and that they have admitted this.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified (S-2, 05:58:03) that Kirk's own security team removed him from the scene while administering first aid and made a hasty transport to Timpanogos Hospital.
  - S-2 (05:58:03) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "Mr. Kirk's detail, his own security team removed him from the scene whilst administering first aid and they conducted what would be called a hasty transport to Timpanogos Hospital."

### C-3999 (ep 46)
- **Claim:** The host says officials claimed Tyler Robinson confessed and turned himself in and that his father said he was guilty, which she says is not what happened.
- **Record:** Agents Hull (S-3, 01:51:41) and Davis (S-5, 02:50:14; S-4, 37:16) testified that Robinson turned himself in on September 11, 2025, contrary to the host's denial.
  - S-3 (01:51:41) (testimony, Agent Hull, former Utah State Bureau of Investigation agent (witness; Rev also renders Holt/Hall)): "I believe he turned himself in on September 11th."
  - S-5 (02:50:14) (testimony, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "So September 11th was when he turned himself in. It would've been September 11th."
  - S-4 (37:16) (testimony, SBI Agent Brian Davis (witness)): "Tyler Robinson."

### C-4001 (ep 46)
- **Claim:** The host says officials said Tyler Robinson was arrested wearing a maroon shirt and that this was not true.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7, 07:56:00) finds Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office wearing a maroon shirt and jeans, and Sgt. Faumuina (S-5, 05:00:28) testified he wore a maroon top when arrested.
  - S-7 (07:56:00) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "At approximately 9:00 PM, Mr. Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office. He was wearing a maroon shirt and jeans."
  - S-5 (05:00:28) (testimony, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "I believe it was a maroon top."

### C-5296 (ep 59)
- **Claim:** The host says Brian Harpole told Shawn Ryan that there were no drones because the university denied TPUSA's request to fly them.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, p. 48) records that on the Shawn Ryan Podcast Harpole said his team had drones but did not use them because of FAA restrictions tied to Provo Airport airspace, not because the university denied a request.
  - S-12 p. 47 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "appearance on the Shawn Ryan Podcast, titled “Brian Harpole – Groundbreaking Evidence From Charlie Kirk’s Head of Security / SRS #254."
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Harpole also stated that although his team had drones, they did not use them during the event because of FAA restrictions related to Provo Airport airspace."

### C-5414 (ep 60)
- **Claim:** The host says Erika Kirk's plane (N102DZ), carrying a priest, Erika Kirk, Tyler Bowyer and Elizabeth McCoy, left at 2:19 p.m. ('it's an hour and 5 minutes') and arrived in Provo at 4:36 p.m., and that she was told Andrew Kolvet was on one of the two planes.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-13) shows N102DZ leaving Scottsdale at 20:31:57Z (13:31 MST / 14:31 MDT) and landing at Provo at 21:30:43Z (15:30 MDT), then departing Provo at 22:10:17Z (16:10 MDT) for Camarillo, so it did not arrive in Provo at 4:36 p.m.
  - S-13 hex a00c85, 20:31:57Z-22:10:17Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a00c85 N102DZ: takeoff 20:31:57Z KSDL; landing 21:30:43Z at 40.2237,-111.7267 (KPVU); takeoff 22:10:17Z KPVU; landing 23:47:53Z at 34.2137,-119.0895 (KCMA)"

### C-7397 (ep 82)
- **Claim:** In the clip, the speakers say Tyler Robinson's parents recognized the released photo, a family friend sat with him, he admitted it and they got him to drive up to Orem to turn himself in.
- **Record:** Agent Davis testified (S-4, 37:00) that Robinson came to the Washington County Sheriff's Office (St. George) with his parents and a family friend, not that he drove up to Orem to turn himself in.
  - S-4 (37:00) (testimony, SBI Agent Brian Davis (witness)): "he'd come there with his parents and a family friend."

### C-7894 (ep 88)
- **Claim:** The host says it is not debated that it is Tyler Robinson's rifle but that there is no evidence that the rifle was fired.
- **Record:** The ATF report read into the record (S-5, 05:29:01) identifies the cartridge case found in the rifle recovered on the UVU campus as fired in that rifle (the bullet comparison itself was inconclusive).
  - S-5 (05:29:01) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): ""The Exhibit 2 cartridge case was identified as having been fired in the Exhibit 1 rifle.""
  - S-5 (05:28:45) (testimony, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "Is the one that we found on campus."

### C-7938 (ep 88)
- **Claim:** The host says, in agreement with the comment, that there is no evidence Charlie was even shot.
- **Record:** Judge Graf's bindover ruling (S-7, 08:16:02) finds the bullet struck Kirk and did not exit his body, and Sgt. Faumuina testified (S-5, 05:30:42) bullet fragments were recovered from Kirk at autopsy.
  - S-7 (08:16:02) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "the bullet traveled anatomically from front to back and downward after striking Mr. Kirk. The projectile did not exit his body."
  - S-5 (05:30:42) (testimony, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "Exhibit 6 is the bullet jacket fragment, as well as some other fragments that were recovered from Mr. Kirk at the time of autopsy."

### C-8073 (ep 90)
- **Claim:** Host references a search warrant indicating Tyler Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office at 10:26 p.m. with his parents.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7, 07:56:00) finds, and Agent Davis testified (S-4, 36:51; S-5, 02:53:36), that Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office at about 9:00 p.m., not 10:26 p.m.
  - S-7 (07:56:00) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "At approximately 9:00 PM, Mr. Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office. He was wearing a maroon shirt and jeans."
  - S-4 (36:51) (testimony, SBI Agent Brian Davis (witness)): "I was told he arrived there about 9:00 PM that evening."
  - S-5 (02:53:36) (testimony, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "Yes, that's correct."

### C-9986 (ep 116)
- **Claim:** The host says there is no evidence that the recovered rifle was fired on September 10 and that this, in her belief, is why the bomb dogs did not locate it.
- **Record:** Judge Graf found evidence (S-7, 08:22:41) that Robinson fired the Mauser .30-06 from the Losee roof toward Kirk, and the ATF identified the fired case in the rifle as fired in it (S-5, 05:29:01).
  - S-7 (08:22:41) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "defendant positioned himself on the roof of the Losee building and discharged a Mauser .30-06 rifle in the direction of Charlie Kirk."
  - S-5 (05:29:01) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): ""The Exhibit 2 cartridge case was identified as having been fired in the Exhibit 1 rifle.""

### C-10617 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** The host says her biggest claim was that Tyler Robinson did not step foot onto campus that day, which is what he told his family, namely that he was around campus but not physically on it.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified video shows Robinson returning to campus on foot just after 10:00 AM on Sept 10 (S-3 00:48:03, 00:51:02), and the bindover ruling finds he walked on and then off campus (S-7 07:50:48).
  - S-3 (00:48:03) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "Just after 10:00 AM."
  - S-3 (00:51:02) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "This is a video of Mr. Robinson returning back to campus the same way that he did previously on foot."
  - S-7 (07:50:48) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "He then walked back down the stairs and off campus."

### C-10954 (ep 128)
- **Claim:** Robert O'Neill says on Newsmax that nobody started first aid on Charlie Kirk and that people were more concerned with getting SIM cards out of cameras, removing cameras and getting Kirk off the X.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified that Kirk's own security team removed him while giving first aid (S-2 05:58:03), contrary to 'no one started doing first aid'.
  - S-2 (05:58:03) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "Mr. Kirk's detail, his own security team removed him from the scene whilst administering first aid and they conducted what would be called a hasty transport to Timpanogos Hospital."

### C-11330 (ep 131)
- **Claim:** In a Newsmax audio clip, Rob O'Neill states the shot "went left to right, not from front to back," and asserts an exit wound on Charlie Kirk's left-hand side.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling finds the bullet went front to back, only slightly left to right, and did not exit Kirk's body (S-7 07:46:29).
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The trajectory of the bullet was anatomically from front to back, slightly left to right and downward."
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The bullet did not exit Mr. Kirk's body, but came to rest and tissues in his upper back left of the middle line."

### C-11451 (ep 133)
- **Claim:** The host says only two people were detained after the shooting, George Zinn, whom she says was detained because he said he did it, and Zachariah Qureshi, who was released without explanation.
- **Record:** Lead agent Hull testified 'several people were detained for questioning and interviewed throughout the investigation' (S-3 01:45:41), contrary to 'only two people were detained'.
  - S-3 (01:45:41) (testimony, Agent Hull, former Utah State Bureau of Investigation agent (witness; Rev also renders Holt/Hall)): "There were several people who were detained for questioning and who were interviewed throughout the investigation."

### C-11509 (ep 134) · keeps revised-by-host
- **Claim:** The host says that Tyler Robinson's defense filed a memorandum highlighting injuries to Charlie Kirk found at autopsy, including that his heart and lungs hemorrhaged after a bullet hit his neck (she corrects the filer later in the episode).
- **Record:** The ruling records that the State filed its bindover memorandum on July 28, 2026 and the defense filed its first briefing on Aug 11, 2026 (S-7 07:42:28), so the memorandum discussed the day before this July 29 episode was the State's (the host later corrected this herself).
  - S-7 (07:42:28) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The state filed its memorandum in support of a preliminary hearing bind over on July 28th, 2026. Defendant filed his preliminary hearing briefing on August 11th, 2026, and the state filed its reply memorandum on August 18th, 2026."

### C-11564 (ep 134)
- **Claim:** The host says there is no evidence of a screwdriver, referring to what Blake Neff cites, and asks where it is in the evidence.
- **Record:** Faumuina testified a screwdriver was found as evidence (S-5 04:35:59), it appears in State's Exhibit 3.1 beside evidence tag five (S-2 02:13:57), and the FBI lab examined it as item 8 (S-3 08:07:21).
  - S-5 (04:35:59) (testimony, Sgt. Jennifer Faumuina, crime scene/evidence supervisor (witness)): "One item of evidence that was found was a screwdriver."
  - S-2 (02:13:57) (exhibit, prosecutor Chad Grunander): "Now, on this exhibit, Exhibit 3.1, there's an evidence tag, yellow number five."
  - S-3 (08:07:21) (testimony, Ms. Bakker, FBI Laboratory forensic examiner (witness)): "Correct. Yes."

### C-11581 (ep 134)
- **Claim:** The host says the public has seen no evidence of a screwdriver or a gun on the Losee rooftop.
- **Record:** Bagley testified to a red-and-black screwdriver on the Losee rooftop and identified a photo of it (S-2 01:43:39, 01:44:10), and Faumuina confirmed it was collected from the roof (S-3 05:02:19), so 'no evidence of a screwdriver' is contradicted (the gun half is not settled).
  - S-2 (01:43:39) (testimony, Officer Bagley, UVU Police (witness; Rev label 'Richard Novak')): "It was a red and black screwdriver."
  - S-2 (01:44:10) (exhibit, Officer Bagley, UVU Police (witness; Rev label 'Richard Novak')): "That's a picture of the screwdriver that I saw that day on top of the rooftop of the Losee building."
  - S-3 (05:02:19) (testimony, Sgt. Jennifer Faumuina, Utah DPS State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "Yes."

### C-11663 (ep 135)
- **Claim:** Per the surgeon YouTube comment, the trajectory from a rooftop shooter is incompatible with the wound; to affect up to C2, an upward trajectory from the point of entry on the neck would be required.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling summarising the medical findings gives the bullet path as front to back, slightly left to right and downward (S-7, 07:46:29), not upward.
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The trajectory of the bullet was anatomically from front to back, slightly left to right and downward."

### C-11954 (ep 139)
- **Claim:** The host says she still believes what she reported in October of last year, that Tyler Robinson never stepped foot onto the UVU campus, and that the claim has not been debunked.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified UVU surveillance video shows Robinson visiting the campus four times on September 10, including returning on foot just after 10:00 a.m. (S-3, 00:48:03; S-2, 06:35:55).
  - S-3 (00:48:03) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "Just after 10:00 AM."
  - S-2 (06:35:55) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "That is correct, yes."
  - S-3 (00:24:01) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "It does, yes."

### C-13026 (ep 151)
- **Claim:** The host says Officer Bagley testified that the figure in the black American-flag shirt is the same person as the one in the maroon shirt because both wore sneakers, and calls that implausible on a college campus.
- **Record:** Bagley testified he could not identify the rooftop person's shoes (S-2 04:14:09); the shoe-based same-person identification was Agent Hull's testimony (S-3 00:51:25).
  - S-2 (04:14:09) (testimony, Officer Bagley, UVU Police (witness)): "I could not."
  - S-3 (00:51:25) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "The shoes are the same and the images that we have from my viewing and seeing Mr. Robinson on the video, it's the same person."

### C-13095 (ep 152)
- **Claim:** The host says Officer Christopher Bagley is the officer used at the preliminary hearing, that he found the screwdriver on the rooftop, and that the screwdriver was the state's only link to Tyler Robinson.
- **Record:** Bagley did find the screwdriver on the Losee roof, but the ruling found FBI testing supported inclusion of Robinson's DNA on the towel, rifle, casings and cartridges too, so it was not the only link (S-7 07:47:33, 07:56:00).
  - S-7 (07:47:33) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "Officer Bagley found a red and black screwdriver approximately 10 to 15 feet west of the guardrail on the roof of the Losee Center."
  - S-7 (07:56:00) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The FBI performed DNA testing on the screwdriver, towel, rifle, spent casings, and three unfired cartridges found near the scene. The testing provided support for inclusion of Mr. Robinson's DNA on each item."

### C-13097 (ep 152)
- **Claim:** The host says the screwdriver was proven not to tell us it is Tyler Robinson's DNA, though she declines to go into it that day.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling says FBI DNA analysis places Robinson's DNA on the screwdriver with a likelihood ratio exceeding 30 quintillion (S-7 08:08:34); nothing shows it was proven not his.
  - S-7 (08:08:34) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "the FBI DNA analysis places defendant's DNA on a screwdriver recovered at the crime scene with a likelihood ratio exceeding 30 quintrillion."
  - S-7 (07:56:00) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The FBI performed DNA testing on the screwdriver, towel, rifle, spent casings, and three unfired cartridges found near the scene. The testing provided support for inclusion of Mr. Robinson's DNA on each item."

### C-13218 (ep 153)
- **Claim:** The host says UVU has a grand total of 24 police officers available to cover its campus.
- **Record:** Asked how many officers UVU police normally employ, Officer Bagley testified 15 (S-2 02:44:31), not 24.
  - S-2 (02:44:31) (testimony, Officer Bagley, UVU Police (witness)): "Employ, there was 15."

### C-13407 (ep 155)
- **Claim:** The host says Charlie Kirk did not have a neck of steel, that a .300 round would have passed through it, that he simply was not shot by a .300 and potentially was not shot at all, and that she thinks an explosive is much more likely.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling finds Charlie Kirk died of a gunshot wound to the neck and that bullet fragments recovered from his body were a .30-caliber class round (S-7 07:46:29).
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "Mr. Kirk died by a gunshot wound to his neck. Forensic examination of bullet fragments found in Mr. Kirk's body revealed the bullet to be a 30 caliber class round."

## corroborated (522)

### C-67 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** Netanyahu claimed on Fox News that Charlie Kirk wrote him a letter on May 2 expressing support for Israel.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-42) reports Netanyahu said Kirk wrote him a letter on May 2 and quoted its line about advocating for Israel being one of his greatest joys as a Christian.
  - S-42 para 166 (tier2_attributed, Benjamin Netanyahu): ""He wrote me a letter on May 2 this year. He said, ‘One of my greatest joys as a Christian is advocating for Israel and forming alliances to defend Judeo-Christian civilization,’""

### C-69 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** In the Fox News clip, Netanyahu says Charlie Kirk was unbelievably excited to walk in the footsteps of Jesus in Israel and valued the bond between America and Israel.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-42) quotes Netanyahu saying Kirk 'was unbelievably excited to walk in the footsteps of Jesus here.'
  - S-42 para 157 (tier2_attributed, Benjamin Netanyahu): ""He was a defender of our common Judeo-Christian civilization. He was unbelievably excited to walk in the footsteps of Jesus here."

### C-114 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** The host says TMZ has footage, which she shows, of a man at 8:07 AM on September 10, wearing a maroon shirt, walking outside university grounds near the UVU campus.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-63) reports surveillance video appears to show the alleged shooter walking toward UVU's campus hours before the shooting; the 8:07 a.m. time and maroon-shirt details rest only on unconfirmed TMZ-sourced facts.
  - S-63 para 156 (tier2_reported, Fox News): "New surveillance video appears to show the man authorities say assassinated Charlie Kirk walking toward Utah Valley University’s campus hours before he allegedly carried out the killing."

### C-115 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** Governor Cox stated Robinson arrived at UVU campus at 8:29 AM in a gray Dodge Challenger wearing maroon shirt and shorts.
- **Record:** Sgt. Faumuina (S-5 05:17:16) testified Robinson's vehicle was a gray Dodge Challenger, and the bindover ruling (S-7 07:49:47) found he walked from the garage toward the amphitheater in a maroon short-sleeve shirt and shorts; the 8:29 AM time appears only in the State's closing argument.
  - S-5 (05:17:16) (testimony, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "That vehicle is, say, a gray-colored Dodge Challenger."
  - S-7 (07:49:47) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "A person identified as Mr. Robinson exited the Charger and walked on foot out of the parking garage toward the outdoor amphitheater on campus. He was wearing a maroon short sleeve shirt, shorts, a hat, and a backpack."
  - S-3 (00:47:48) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "It's a maroon color T-shirt with gray or olive colored shorts and then converse shoes."

### C-117 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** The host says TMZ released doorbell-camera footage at 11:49 AM on September 10, which she shows, of the shooter in a different outfit and appearing to limp.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified (S-3 00:51:43) that on Robinson's late-morning return before the shooting he wore different clothing and walked with a gait or limp; the TMZ/doorbell source is not addressed.
  - S-3 (00:51:43) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "He is wearing different clothing and he also is walking with a gait or a limp in this video."

### C-118 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** The host says Charlie Kirk was shot at 12:23 PM on September 10, 2025.
- **Record:** CNN via KEYT (S-21) reports Kirk was shot around 12:23 p.m. on September 10, 2025.
  - S-21 para 156 (tier2_reported, CNN (via KEYT)): "Kirk was shot around 12:23 p.m."
  - S-21 para 134 (tier2_reported, CNN (via KEYT)): "Kirk was killed September 10, 2025, when he was fatally shot in front of about 3,000 people during a Turning Point USA event at Utah Valley University in Orem."

### C-119 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** The host says footage shows that, at the same minute as the 12:23 shot and as the crowd ducks, the shooter runs across the rooftop.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified (S-3 00:55:27) the individual stayed at the roof edge until the 12:23:28 shot then moved north across the building, and Sgt. Faumuina (S-5 04:45:51) that video showed the shooter run from the southwest to the northeast corner of the roof.
  - S-3 (00:55:27) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "Just until the reported shot was fired at 12:23:28. The individual then stands up and then moves across the building to the north."
  - S-5 (04:45:51) (testimony, Sgt. Jennifer Faumuina, crime scene/evidence supervisor (witness)): "it showed who we believe was the shooter on the rooftop run from the southwest corner all the way to the northeast corner, jump down"

### C-124 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** The host says authorities say the weapon was found in the woods wrapped in a dark towel, and that the photo they gave of it showed it fully assembled.
- **Record:** Sgt. Faumuina testified (S-3 05:03:16) a long gun wrapped in a dark-colored towel was found in the wooded area, and the ruling (S-7 08:24:13) found the rifle was concealed in a dense wooded area wrapped in a dark towel.
  - S-3 (05:03:16) (testimony, Sgt. Jennifer Faumuina, Utah DPS State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "What was found there was a long gun that was wrapped in a dark-colored towel."
  - S-7 (08:24:13) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "Following the shooting, the Mauser .30-06 rifle was concealed in a dense wooded area on UVU's campus, wrapped inside a dark towel."

### C-125 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** The host says she has been told that a screwdriver was found on the roof.
- **Record:** Officer Bagley testified (S-2 01:43:39) he found a red and black screwdriver on the roof, and the ruling (S-7 07:47:33) found it 10-15 feet west of the guardrail on the Losee Center roof.
  - S-2 (01:43:39) (testimony, Officer Bagley, UVU Police (witness; Rev label 'Richard Novak')): "It was a red and black screwdriver."
  - S-7 (07:47:33) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "Officer Bagley found a red and black screwdriver approximately 10 to 15 feet west of the guardrail on the roof of the Losee Center."

### C-126 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** The host says the FBI released photos of the shooter in a stairwell, in what she calls outfit number two, and that the location of the stairwell and the timeline are not given.
- **Record:** Agent Hull agreed (S-3 01:53:44) that the person-of-interest images released to the press were the ones from the stairwell.
  - S-3 (01:53:44) (testimony, Agent Hull, former Utah State Bureau of Investigation agent (witness; Rev also renders Holt/Hall)): "Correct."

### C-129 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** The host says it was communicated that Tyler Robinson was wearing the maroon outfit when he was apprehended, 33 hours after the shooting, which she finds odd given the reported outfit change in between.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7 07:56:00) found Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office about 9:00 PM on September 11 wearing a maroon shirt (with jeans, not shorts).
  - S-7 (07:56:00) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "At approximately 9:00 PM, Mr. Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office. He was wearing a maroon shirt and jeans."

### C-136 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** Governor Cox says the roommate showed several Discord messages to investigators and allowed them to take photos of the screen as each message was shown.
- **Record:** Agent Davis testified (S-5 02:51:08-02:51:15) that Exhibit 16.2 shows the Discord messages Lance Twiggs described, and in his recorded statement (S-5 Exhibit 16, 02:21:58) Twiggs identified the photographs as his own cracked phone's screen showing Discord, and confirmed they were the same messages.
  - S-5 (02:51:08) (testimony, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "These are the Discord messages that Lance had talked about."
  - S-5 (02:51:15) (testimony, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "And then these images are shown to Lance during his second interview and he confirms that these are those same messages"
  - S-5 (02:21:58) (exhibit, Lance Twiggs (sworn recorded statement, State's Exhibit 16)): "Yeah, this is Discord."

### C-137 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** Governor Cox says messages from the contact "Tyler" stated a need to retrieve a rifle from a drop point, leaving the rifle in a bush, visually watching the area where it was left, and having left it wrapped in a towel.
- **Record:** The ruling (S-7 07:52:46) recites texts saying he left his rifle wrapped in a towel in a bush where he changed outfits, and the texts quoted in the Information (S-1 p. 8) include 'I had planned to grab my rifle from my drop point' and 'the rifle wrapped in a towel.'
  - S-7 (07:52:46) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The texter explained that he was still in Orem because he had left his rifle wrapped in a towel in a bush where he changed outfits."
  - S-1 p. 8 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "I had planned to grab my rifle from my drop point shortly after, but most of that side of town got locked down."
  - S-1 p. 8 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "only thing I left was the rifle wrapped in a towel."

### C-138 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** Governor Cox says the messages also refer to engraving bullets, a scope and the rifle being unique.
- **Record:** Texts read by Agent Davis (S-5 02:47:11) ask 'Remember how I was engraving bullets?', the ruling (S-7 07:52:46) recites a text about a $2,000 scope, and the Information (S-1 p. 9) quotes a text calling the rifle 'very unique.'
  - S-5 (02:47:11) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): ""Remember how I was engraving bullets? The fucking messages are mostly a big meme. If I see, Notices bulge, UwU, on Fox News, I might have a stroke.""
  - S-7 (07:52:46) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): ""Judging from today, I would say grandpa's gun does just fine. IDK. "I think it was a $2,000 scope or a 2K scope."
  - S-1 p. 9 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "the feds released a photo of the rifle, and it is very unique. Hes calling me rn, not answering."

### C-139 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** Governor Cox says messages from the contact "Tyler" also mentioned that he had changed outfits.
- **Record:** Agent Davis read the text 'I had to leave it in a bush where I changed outfits' (S-5 02:46:40), and the ruling (S-7 08:24:13) cites the same text messages.
  - S-5 (02:46:40) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "I had to leave it in a bush where I changed outfits. Didn't have the ability or time to bring it with.""
  - S-7 (08:24:13) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "text messages attributed to him in which he stated he had concealed his rifle by leaving it in a bush where he changed outfits."

### C-141 (ep 2)
- **Claim:** Discord issued a statement saying they found no evidence that the suspect planned the incident or promoted violence on Discord.
- **Record:** S-16 reports Discord's statement that it found 'no evidence that the suspect planned this incident or promoted violence on Discord.'
  - S-16 para 37 (tier2_attributed, Discord): ""no evidence that the suspect planned this incident or promoted violence on Discord.""

### C-156 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** The host says George Zinn, a 71-year-old man, jumped up after the Kirk shooting implying he did it, was arrested, and now faces obstruction of justice charges.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12 p. 21) records that at 12:26 p.m. attendee George Zinn was in custody after allegedly admitting involvement and was later determined not to be a suspect; his age and obstruction charge are not covered.
  - S-12 p. 21 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "12:26 p.m. UVUPD reported a suspect, George Zinn, an event attendee, was in custody after allegedly admitting involvement in the shooting. It was later determined he was not a suspect."

### C-157 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the article the host reads, George Zinn, accused of obstructing justice, was also booked in connection with images on his cell phone related to child abuse following the obstruction arrest.
- **Record:** KSL (S-30) reports George Zinn, arrested after the shooting, was charged with obstruction of justice and sexual exploitation of a minor and booked into the Utah County Jail.
  - S-30 para 12 (tier2_reported, KSL (photo caption)): "George Zinn, 71, who was arrested after Charlie Kirk was shot at Utah Valley University in Orem on Sept. 10, was charged this week with obstruction of justice and sexual exploitation of a minor."
  - S-30 para 26 (tier2_reported, KSL): "Zinn was released from the hospital on Monday and then booked into the Utah County Jail."

### C-162 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, Tyler James Robinson surrendered to the Washington County Sheriff's Office on September 11, 2025, approximately 33 hours after the shooting.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 3) states that after an approximately 33-hour manhunt Robinson surrendered at the Washington County Sheriff's Office on the evening of September 11, 2025, and Agent Davis confirmed (S-5 02:53:36) his arrival about 9:00 PM.
  - S-1 p. 3 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Over the next approximately 33 hours, police conducted a manhunt for the shooter until the evening of September 11, 2025, when Tyler James Robinson surrendered to police at the Washington County Sheriff’s Office."
  - S-5 (02:53:36) (testimony, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "Yes, that's correct."

### C-163 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the probable cause statement overview, the host says it alleges Robinson hid the gun, discarded the clothing he wore when he fired the rifle, and told his roommate to delete incriminating text messages and not to speak with police.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 3, as also read at S-11 06:14) alleges Robinson hid the gun, discarded the clothing he wore when he fired, and told his roommate to delete incriminating texts and not talk to police.
  - S-1 p. 3 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "After shooting Mr. Kirk, Robinson hid the gun (Count 3), discarded the clothing he wore when he fired the rifle (Count 4)"
  - S-1 p. 3 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "told his lover/roommate to delete incriminating text messages"
  - S-11 (06:14) (charging_allegation, Jeff Gray, Utah County Attorney): "Robinson hid the gun, discarded the clothing he wore when he fired the rifle and told his roommate to delete incriminating text messages and not talk to police."

### C-165 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says that after the shooting a police officer immediately looked upward and realized he had heard a rifle, knowing the sound came from the rooftops.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 4) states the officer, believing from its sound that the shot came from a rifle, looked for sniper positions, and Officer Bagley testified (S-2 01:40:24) he realized it was a rifle shot.
  - S-1 p. 4 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Believing the shot came from a rifle because of its sound, he looked for potential sniper positions."
  - S-2 (01:40:24) (testimony, Officer Bagley, UVU Police (witness; Rev label 'Richard Novak')): "I realized that the shot I heard was more of a rifle shot and not a pistol shot."

### C-166 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, a surveillance camera covering the roof recorded an individual in dark clothing crossing the railing onto the roof at approximately 12:15pm; the host adds that, by her timeline, this is about eight minutes before the 12:23pm shot.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 5) states the roof camera recorded an individual in dark clothing crossing the railing from the public walkway and dropping onto the roof at approximately 12:15 p.m.
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "it recorded an individual dressed in dark clothing cross the railing from the public walkway and drop onto the roof at approximately 12:15 p.m."

### C-167 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says the roof camera captured the individual running across the roof and low crawling to the spot where the suspected sniper had dropped into a prone shooting position, and that after a short time matching the known time of the shot the individual arose and ran northeast.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 5) states the camera captured the individual running across the roof and low-crawling to the prone position, then, at a time matching the shot, rising and running northeast.
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "the camera again captured the individual running across the roof"
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "After a sho rt time, which matches the known time of the shot, the individual arose and ran across the roof to the northeast."

### C-169 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, UVU surveillance showed the suspect entering campus from the north at approximately 11:51am wearing a black shirt with an American flag, a dark cap and large sunglasses.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 5) states the suspect entered campus from the north at about 11:51 a.m. in a black shirt with an American flag, a dark baseball cap and large sunglasses.
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "At approximately 11:51 a.m., the suspect entered campus from the north."
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "He is seen wearing a black shirt with an American flag in its center, a dark baseball cap, and large sunglasses."

### C-170 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says surveillance showed the suspect keeping his head down and walking with an unusual gait, with very little bending in his right leg, which the document calls consistent with a rifle hidden in his pants.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 5) states the suspect kept his head down and walked with very little bending in his right leg, 'consistent with a rifle being hidden in his pants.'
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "the suspect keeps his head down and rarely raises his head enough to get a clear image of his face."
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "The suspect walks with very little bending in his right leg – consistent with a rifle being hidden in his pants."

### C-174 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says the suspect was seen climbing down from the roof, appearing to drop the item he carried as he hit the ground in a controlled fall, then picking it up and running toward the northeast end of campus.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 5, read at S-11 13:41) states the suspect climbed down from the roof, appeared to drop the item in a controlled fall, picked it up and ran toward the northeast end of campus.
  - S-11 (13:41) (charging_allegation, Jeff Gray, Utah County Attorney): "The suspect is then seen climbing down from the roof. He appears to drop the item he was carrying as he hits the ground in a controlled fall."
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "He appears to drop the item he was carrying as he hits the ground in a controlled fall. He then picks up the item and runs toward the northeast end of campus."

### C-175 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, investigators found a bolt-action rifle wrapped in a towel in a wooded area at the northeast end of campus, with one spent round and three unspent rounds, and no shell casings were found on the roof.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 5) states investigators found a towel-wrapped bolt-action rifle in the wooded area containing one spent and three unspent rounds, and no shell casings on the roof.
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "In that wooded area, investigators found a bolt-action rifle wrapped in a towel."
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "The rifle contained one spent round and three unspent rounds."
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "no shell casings were found on the roof, suggesting a bolt-action rather than an auto-loading weapon, and only a single round was fired."

### C-176 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, inscriptions on the cartridges read 'Hey fascist catch', 'Oh Bella cow', and 'if you read this you are gay Lmo'.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 6) quotes the cartridge etching 'If you Read This, You Are GAY Lmao', and Sgt. Faumuina read the exhibit engravings 'O Bella Ciao' and '...a fascist' (S-5 04:57:36-04:58:19), which the host renders as 'Oh Bella cow' and 'Hey fascist catch.'
  - S-1 p. 6 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Fourth cartridge: If you Read This, You Are GAY Lmao"
  - S-5 (04:58:19) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "O Bella Ciao."
  - S-5 (04:57:36) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "A fascist."

### C-177 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, DNA consistent with Robinson was found on the rifle trigger and other parts of the rifle, plus the fired cartridge casing, two of three unfired cartridges and the towel.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 6) states DNA consistent with Robinson's was on the trigger, other rifle parts, the fired casing, two of three unfired cartridges and the towel; the ruling (S-7 07:56:00) found FBI testing supported inclusion of his DNA on those items.
  - S-1 p. 6 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "DNA consistent with Robinson’s was found on the trigger, other parts of the rifle, the fired cartridge casing, two of the three unfired cartridges, and the towel."
  - S-7 (07:56:00) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The FBI performed DNA testing on the screwdriver, towel, rifle, spent casings, and three unfired cartridges found near the scene. The testing provided support for inclusion of Mr. Robinson's DNA on each item."

### C-178 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says police were unable to immediately locate the shooter, so they published photos of the shooter from their surveillance and asked for the public's help.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 6) states that, unable to locate the shooter immediately, law enforcement published UVU surveillance photos of him and asked for the public's help.
  - S-1 p. 6 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "they published photos of the shooter from the UVU surveillance cameras and asked for the public’s help to identify him."

### C-179 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, Robinson's mother told police she saw the news photo of the shooter on September 11 and thought it looked like her son; she called her son, then expressed concern to her husband.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 6) states that on September 11 Robinson's mother saw the shooter's photo in the news, thought it looked like her son, and expressed that concern to her husband.
  - S-1 p. 6 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson’s mother saw the photo of the shooter in the news and thought the shooter looked like her son."
  - S-1 p. 6 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson’s mother expressed concern to her husband that the suspected shooter looked like Robinson. Robinson’s father agreed."

### C-180 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says Robinson's mother called her son after seeing the news photo and that he said he was at home sick and had also been home sick the previous day, September 10, when the shooting occurred.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 6) states that when his mother called, Robinson said he was at home sick and had also been home sick on September 10.
  - S-1 p. 6 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "He said he was at home sick and that he had also been at home sick on September 10 th."

### C-181 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, Robinson's mother told police he had become increasingly political over the past year, leaning more left, more pro-gay, and more trans-rights oriented, with difficult discussions with his father.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 7) states his mother said he had become more political and left-leaning, more pro-gay and trans-rights oriented, leading to discussions especially with his father.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson had become more political and had started to lean more to the left – becoming more pro-gay and trans-rights oriented."
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "This resulted in several discussions with family members, but especially between Robinson and his father, who have very different political views."

### C-182 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, Robinson's mother told police he began dating his roommate, a biological male transitioning genders.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 7) states his mother said Robinson began dating his roommate, a biological male who was transitioning genders.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson began to date his roommate, a biological male who was transitioning genders."

### C-183 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says Robinson's mother told police that in one conversation before the shooting Robinson mentioned Charlie Kirk would hold an event at UVU, which he called a stupid venue, and accused Kirk of spreading hate.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 7) states Robinson mentioned Kirk's UVU event, called it a 'stupid venue', and accused Kirk of spreading hate.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson mentioned that Charlie Kir k would be holding an event at UVU, which Robinson said was a “stupid venue” for the event. Robinson accused Kirk of spreading hate."

### C-184 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, Robinson's father reported that he agreed the shooter photo looked like his son and believed the rifle matched a rifle gifted to his son; he asked for a photo of that rifle and Robinson did not respond.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 7) states the father believed the rifle matched one gifted to his son, asked for a photo of it, and got no response.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "He also believed that the rifle that police suspected the shooter used matched a rifle that was given to his son as a gift."
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson’s father contacted his son and asked him to send a photo of the rifle. Robinson did not respond."

- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 7) states Robinson implied to his parents that he was the shooter, said he couldn't go to jail and just wanted to end it.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson implied that he was the shooter and stated that he couldn’t go to jail and just wanted to end it."

### C-186 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says that when asked why he did it, Robinson explained that there was too much evil and that Charlie Kirk spreads too much hate.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 7) states Robinson explained there is too much evil and Kirk spreads too much hate.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson explained there is too much evil and the guy [Charlie Kirk] spreads too much hate ."

### C-187 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Per the probable cause statement the host reads, Robinson's parents convinced him not to harm himself, and a family friend who is a retired deputy sheriff facilitated Robinson's surrender with his parents.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 7) states his parents convinced him to speak with a family friend who is a retired deputy sheriff, who met with them and convinced him to turn himself in.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "convinced Robinson to speak with a family friend who is a retired deputy sheriff."
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "the family friend met with Robinson and his parents and convinced Robinson to turn himself in."

### C-188 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says Robinson was told to bring in any clothes he used so police would not have to search his parents' property.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 7) states the family friend told Robinson it would be best to bring all evidence to the sheriff's office to avoid police having to search his parents' home.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "it would be best if he brought all evidence with him to the sheriff’s office to avoid police having to search his parent’s home."

### C-189 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says Robinson replied that he had already disposed of clothes in a different area, and notes that the instruction to bring in his clothes so police would not search his parents' property does not exactly make sense to her because Robinson lived with his boyfriend.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 7) states Robinson replied that he had disposed of the clothes in different areas, and the ruling (S-7 07:55:06) found he told the family friend he had discarded those items.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson replied that he had disposed of the clothes in different areas."
  - S-7 (07:55:06) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "At approximately 8:30 PM, a family friend went to pick up Mr. Robinson and take him to the sheriff's office. He asked Mr. Robinson if he had any property or clothes that he used during the incident. Mr. Robinson responded that he had discarded those items."

### C-194 (ep 3)

  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "On September 10, 2025, the roommate received a text message from Robinson which said, “drop what you are doing, look under my keyboard.”"
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "found a note that stated, “I had the opportunity to take out Charlie Kirk and I’m going to take it.” Police found a photograph of this note."

### C-195 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** The host says the note quoted in the indictment, I had the opportunity to take out Charlie Kirk and I'm going to take it, mixes past and present tense and is not proper English, so it is unclear whether it says I have or I had.
- **Record:** The charging Information (S-1 p. 7) quotes the note as "I had the opportunity to take out Charlie Kirk and I'm going to take it," which is the wording the host reads (the tense commentary is opinion and is not judged).
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "found a note that stated, “I had the opportunity to take out Charlie Kirk and I’m going to take it.” Police found a photograph of this note."

### C-196 (ep 3)
- **Claim:** Reading the indictment, the host says police found a photograph of the note under the keyboard, followed by a further text exchange between Robinson and the roommate.

  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "found a note that stated, “I had the opportunity to take out Charlie Kirk and I’m going to take it.” Police found a photograph of this note."
  - S-1 p. 8 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "After reading the note, the roommate responded, “What?????????????? You’re joking, right????”"

  - S-1 p. 8 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Shouldn’t be long until I can come home, but I gotta grab my rifle still."

  - S-5 (02:44:08) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "And Tyler replies, "A bit over a week, I believe."
  - S-7 (07:53:54) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "When asked how long he had been planning this, the texter responded, "A bit over a week, I believe." The texter directed Mr. Twiggs to delete this exchange."

  - S-5 (02:42:39) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): ""I'm still okay, my love, but I'm stuck in Orem for a little while longer yet. Shouldn't be long until I can come home, but got to grab my rifle still."
  - S-5 (02:42:39) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "To be honest, I had hoped to keep this secret till I died of old age. I am sorry to involve you.""

### C-306 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** The host reads a text exchange in which Lance asks whether Robinson was the one who did it and Robinson answers "I am. I'm sorry," after which the roommate says he thought they caught the person.

  - S-7 (07:53:54) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "When Mr. Twiggs asked, "You weren't the one who did it, right?" The texter responded, " I am. I'm sorry.""

### C-312 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** Per the text messages in the indictment that the host reads, Robinson depicts himself remaining near the UVU scene after the shooting, attempting to retrieve his rifle from a drop point; the host infers from her timeline that he was still there hours later, late in the evening.
- **Record:** Agent Davis read the text about planning to grab the rifle from the drop point before the area was locked down (S-5 02:43:05), and Agent Hull testified (S-2 06:34:55) Robinson was back on campus late that evening into the early hours of September 11.
  - S-5 (02:43:05) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "I had planned to grab my rifle from the drop point shortly after, but most of that, side of town got locked down."
  - S-2 (06:34:55) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "We were able to establish that Mr. Robinson had been on campus approximately four times throughout the day, twice before the shooting, the time of the shooting, and then after the shooting later in the evening into the early hours of the 11th."

### C-313 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** The host reads a text in which Robinson says he had been planning this for a bit over a week, could get really close to the rifle but a squad car was parked by the drop-off spot that he thought had been swept.
- **Record:** Agent Davis read the texts (S-5 02:44:08): 'A bit over a week, I believe' and 'I can get close to it, but there is a squad car parked right by it. I think they already swept that spot.'
  - S-5 (02:44:08) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "And Tyler replies, "A bit over a week, I believe."
  - S-5 (02:44:08) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "I can get close to it, but there is a squad car parked right by it. I think they already swept that spot, but I don't want to chance it.""

### C-314 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** The host says a Robinson text wonders whether the rifle had a serial number and says it would not trace to him, which she finds strange from someone who had planned for a week, noting he had just said in writing that it was his grandfather's.
- **Record:** Agent Davis's reading (S-5 02:46:40) and the ruling (S-7 07:52:46) quote 'IDK if it had a serial number, but it wouldn't trace to me.'
  - S-5 (02:46:40) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): ""IDK if it had a serial number, but it wouldn't trace to me. I worry about prints."
  - S-7 (07:52:46) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "IDEK if it had a serial number, but it wouldn't trace to me. "I worry about prints. I might have to abandon it and hope they don't find prints.""

### C-315 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** Robinson's text states he changed outfits and left the rifle (referred to as 'Prince') wrapped in a towel in a bush.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7 07:52:46) and the text exhibit read by Agent Davis (S-5 02:46:40) quote the texter leaving the rifle wrapped in a towel in a bush where he changed outfits, and the Information (S-1 p. 8) quotes 'only thing I left was the rifle wrapped in a towel'.
  - S-7 (07:52:46) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The texter explained that he was still in Orem because he had left his rifle wrapped in a towel in a bush where he changed outfits."
  - S-5 (02:46:40) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "I had to leave it in a bush where I changed outfits. Didn't have the ability or time to bring it with.""
  - S-1 p. 8 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "only thing I left was the rifle wrapped in a towel."

### C-321 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** The host says Discord is going to say later that the messages were not coordinated on its server.
- **Record:** S-16 reports Discord said it found no evidence the suspect planned the incident or promoted violence on Discord.
  - S-16 para 37 (tier2_attributed, Discord): ""no evidence that the suspect planned this incident or promoted violence on Discord.""

### C-322 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** At the September 11 morning press conference an official says the FBI recovered what it believes was the weapon used in the shooting, a high-powered bolt-action rifle found in a wooded area where the shooter had fled.
- **Record:** Sgt. Faumuina testified the rifle found in the wooded area was a bolt action (S-3 05:03:46; S-5 05:29:15).
  - S-3 (05:03:46) (testimony, Sgt. Jennifer Faumuina, Utah DPS State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "It was a bolt action."
  - S-5 (05:29:15) (testimony, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "That would be the items we found in the gun on scene. And this was the gun that was found in the wooded area."

### C-327 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** The host reads a text in which Robinson tells Lance to delete the exchange because his father wants photos of the rifle, adding that the feds released a photo of the rifle that is very unique and that his father is calling him.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 9) quotes the texts 'my dad wants photos of the rifle', 'the feds released a photo of the rifle, and it is very unique. Hes calling me rn', and 'delete this exchange'.
  - S-1 p. 9 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson: my dad wants photos of the rifle"
  - S-1 p. 9 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "the feds released a photo of the rifle, and it is very unique. Hes calling me rn, not answering."
  - S-1 p. 9 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson: delete this exchange"

### C-328 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** The host says the indictment has the father saying Tyler did not answer texts but did answer the father's call, that the father says Tyler was suicidal, and that they then brought over a neighbor who is a sheriff friend and agreed he should go in.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 7) says Robinson did not answer his father's photo request, implied by phone he planned to take his own life, and was persuaded by a family friend who is a retired deputy sheriff to turn himself in.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson’s father contacted his son and asked him to send a photo of the rifle. Robinson did not respond."
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "However, Robinson’s father spoke on the phone with Robinson. Robinson implied that he planned to take his own life."
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "convinced Robinson to speak with a family friend who is a retired deputy sheriff."
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "the family friend met with Robinson and his parents and convinced Robinson to turn himself in."

### C-330 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** The host reads a text in which Robinson writes that since Trump got into office his dad has been pretty diehard MAGA, which she says gives both the right and the left something to blame and reads as federally written.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 9) and the charging press conference (S-11 24:20) quote the text 'since trump got into office [my dad] has been pretty diehard maga'.
  - S-1 p. 9 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "since trump got into office [my dad] has been pretty diehard maga."
  - S-11 (24:20) (charging_allegation, Jeff Gray, Utah County Attorney): ""Since Trump got into office, my dad has been pretty die-hard MAGA.""

### C-331 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** The host reads a text in which Robinson says he is going to turn himself in willingly and that one of his neighbors, a deputy for the sheriff, is there.
- **Record:** The text exhibit read in court (S-5 02:49:25; S-7 05:06:00) reads 'I'm going to turn myself in willingly. One of my neighbors here is a deputy for the sheriff.'
  - S-5 (02:49:25) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): ""I'm going to turn myself in willingly. One of my neighbors here is a deputy for the sheriff. We hope to keep things sort of quiet here.""
  - S-7 (05:06:00) (exhibit, defense attorney Mr. Novak): ""I'm going to turn myself in willingly. One of my neighbors here is a deputy for the sheriff.""

### C-339 (ep 4)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Donald Trump tells Fox and Friends that, with a high degree of certainty, the suspect is in custody, a statement the host dates to 6:00 a.m. on September 12.
- **Record:** S-52 reports Trump told Fox and Friends on Sept. 12, 'With a high degree of certainty, we have him in custody.'
  - S-52 para 149 (tier2_attributed, Donald Trump): ""With a high degree of certainty, we have him in custody."

### C-396 (ep 5)
- **Claim:** The host says the Robinson and Lance texts have Robinson reporting what he has done minute by minute, mentioning a police sweep, and waiting to retrieve the rifle he was minding.
- **Record:** Text exhibits read by Agent Davis (S-5 02:43:32, 02:45:38) show Robinson telling Twiggs he would attempt to retrieve the rifle unseen and that police had not published anything about finding it; 'minute by minute' and the police sweep are the host's description.
  - S-5 (02:43:32) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "If I'm able to grab my rifle unseen, I will have left no evidence. Going to attempt to retrieve it again."
  - S-5 (02:45:38) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "And Tyler says, "Don't know, they haven't published anything if they have." And then Lance says, "Why did you leave it behind?""

### C-405 (ep 5)
- **Claim:** The host says the official account has Robinson wearing light khaki shorts and a maroon shirt onto campus and then completely changing hours later, and argues that changing only the shirt would have made more sense.
- **Record:** The ruling (S-7 07:49:47) and Hull (S-3 00:47:48) have Robinson arriving in a maroon shirt and shorts (Hull says gray or olive, not khaki), and the Information alleges a person in dark clothing crossed onto the roof at about 12:15 p.m. (S-1 p. 5).
  - S-7 (07:49:47) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "A person identified as Mr. Robinson exited the Charger and walked on foot out of the parking garage toward the outdoor amphitheater on campus. He was wearing a maroon short sleeve shirt, shorts, a hat, and a backpack."
  - S-3 (00:47:48) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "It's a maroon color T-shirt with gray or olive colored shorts and then converse shoes."
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "it recorded an individual dressed in dark clothing cross the railing from the public walkway and drop onto the roof at approximately 12:15 p.m."

### C-424 (ep 5)
- **Claim:** The host says that according to the indictment there were children present who witnessed the shooting.
- **Record:** The Information alleges children were present and visible near the stage at the shooting (S-1 p. 4), and the bindover ruling found children under 14 physically present (S-7 08:31:41).
  - S-1 p. 4 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "(Count 5) and not to talk to police (Count 6). Children were present at the time of the shooting"
  - S-1 p. 4 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Children were visible near Mr. Kirk’s stage when he was shot."
  - S-7 (08:31:41) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "evidence that children younger than 14 were physically present at the event."

  - S-42 para 166 (tier2_attributed, Benjamin Netanyahu): ""He wrote me a letter on May 2 this year. He said, ‘One of my greatest joys as a Christian is advocating for Israel and forming alliances to defend Judeo-Christian civilization,’""

### C-585 (ep 7)
- **Claim:** The host says Erika Kirk publicly forgave Charlie Kirk's killer at the memorial event, which the host describes as a message of complete submission to Christ.
- **Record:** Deseret News (S-64) reports Erika Kirk forgave her husband's accused killer before the millions watching his public memorial (the 'submission to Christ' framing is the host's).
  - S-64 para 83 (tier2_reported, Deseret News): "Cox was there the day she forgave her husband’s accused killer to the millions watching Kirk’s public memorial."

### C-593 (ep 7)
- **Claim:** TPUSA spokesperson Andrew Kolvet publicly stated the absence of an exit wound was likely a miracle after speaking with the surgeon.
- **Record:** S-28 reports Andrew Kolvet wrote on X about the lack of an exit wound and said Kirk's surgeon called it an 'absolute miracle' the bullet did not exit.
  - S-28 para 156 (tier2_attributed, Andrew Kolvet): "Turning Point USA spokesman Andrew Kolvet on Saturday said Charlie Kirk 's surgeon called it an "absolute miracle" that the bullet that killed him didn’t exit his body"
  - S-28 para 157 (tier2_attributed, Andrew Kolvet): ""I want to address some of the discussion about the lack of an exit wound with Charlie."

### C-598 (ep 7)
- **Claim:** FBI Director Kash Patel publicly committed the FBI to investigating every facet of Charlie Kirk's assassination including the possibility of accomplices.
- **Record:** S-16 reports Kash Patel said the FBI was examining the possibility of accomplices, pursuing every lead, and would continue until every question is answered.
  - S-16 para 29 (tier2_attributed, Kash Patel): "FBI Director Kash Patel on Sunday said the agency was looking into the "possibility of accomplices" and "pursuing every lead""
  - S-16 para 31 (tier2_attributed, Kash Patel): "the location from where the shot was taken, the possibility of accomplices, the text message confession and related conversations, Discord chats, the angle of the shot and bullet impact, how the weapon was transported"
  - S-16 para 38 (tier2_attributed, Kash Patel): ""will not rest until justice is served, and our investigation into this assassination will continue until every question is answered.""

### C-599 (ep 7)
- **Claim:** The host reads Kash Patel's tweet saying the FBI is examining theories including how the weapon was transported, hand gestures seen as possible signals near Charlie and visitors to the alleged shooter's residence.
- **Record:** S-16 reports Patel wrote on X that the FBI was examining hand gestures seen as potential signals near Kirk and visitors to the alleged shooter's residence.
  - S-16 para 31 (tier2_attributed, Kash Patel): "hand gestures observed as potential 'signals' near Charlie at the time of his assassination, and visitors to the alleged shooter’s residence in the hours and days leading up to September 10, 2025"

### C-750 (ep 9)
- **Claim:** The host says the indictment states that Tyler Robinson dropped onto the roof at 12:15 p.m.
- **Record:** The Information (S-1 p. 5; read at S-11 12:02) alleges an individual in dark clothing crossed the railing and dropped onto the roof at approximately 12:15 p.m.
  - S-1 p. 5 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "it recorded an individual dressed in dark clothing cross the railing from the public walkway and drop onto the roof at approximately 12:15 p.m."
  - S-11 (12:02) (charging_allegation, Jeff Gray, Utah County Attorney): "it recorded an individual dressed in dark clothing cross the railing from the public walkway and drop onto the roof at approximately 12:15 PM."

### C-751 (ep 9)
- **Claim:** Host's source says the bullet was too fragmented to match Tyler Robinson's rifle.
- **Record:** The ATF report (S-5 exhibit 05:31:18) says the bullet jacket fragment 'cannot be identified or excluded' as fired from the rifle, and ATF examiner Karner testified (S-5 07:12:54, 07:19:31) that damage left too few quality marks for an identification.
  - S-5 (05:31:18) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): ""The Exhibit 6 bullet jacket fragment cannot be identified or excluded as having been fired from the Exhibit 1 rifle"
  - S-5 (07:12:54) (testimony, Samantha Karner, ATF firearm and toolmark examiner (defense witness)): "So because of this lack of quality and quantity of marks to be able to see, it never reached the threshold of sufficient agreement nor sufficient disagreement. So an identification or exclusion could not be rendered."
  - S-5 (07:19:31) (testimony, Samantha Karner, ATF firearm and toolmark examiner (witness)): "that lends to the lack of quality and quantity of marks even to do a comparison. It's almost like missing data points."

### C-839 (ep 10)
- **Claim:** The host restates that Netanyahu phoned Charlie Kirk and invited him to Israel, a point she says she made in her earlier coverage of the Hamptons weekend.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-42) reports Netanyahu said he spoke to Kirk in the week of the assassination and invited him back to Israel.
  - S-42 para 167 (tier2_attributed, Benjamin Netanyahu): "He added that he had spoken to Kirk as recently as this week and invited him back to Israel."

### C-961 (ep 11)
- **Claim:** The host recounts the media narrative that Tyler Robinson's father realized his son was guilty and phoned a friend to help convince him to turn himself in.
