- **Record:** The Salt Lake Tribune (S-18) reports Robinson's parents convinced him to turn himself in, and AP (S-33) cites charging documents saying his father agreed the surveillance image looked like his son, matching the narrative the host recounts.
  - S-33 para 26 (tier2_attributed, charging documents): "Robinson's father reported that when his wife showed him the surveillance image of the suspected shooter in the news, he agreed it looked like their son"
  - S-18 para 14 (tier2_reported, The Salt Lake Tribune): "33 hours after the shooting at Utah Valley University , they convinced their son to turn himself in"

### C-971 (ep 11)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Nate Brooksby says that at 8:02 p.m. a friend who has been retired a few years, whom the host describes as a former detective, called to say he knew who the Charlie Kirk shooter was.
- **Record:** S-61 reports Sheriff Nate Brooksby said a friend, a retired detective who worked for him for years, called him on Sept. 11 to say who the alleged shooter was (the 8:02 p.m. time is not reported).
  - S-61 para 105 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): ""I received a call from a friend of mine who happens to be a retired detective that worked for me for many years," Brooksby said."
  - S-61 para 103 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "Brooksby also recounted how a friend and former deputy called him on Thursday"

### C-974 (ep 11)
- **Claim:** In the Brooksby press-conference clip aired by the host, Brooksby relays his friend's call: the friend said he knows the family through religious association, that Tyler is in Washington County, and that they were working on trying to get him to come in 'involuntarily' (probably 'voluntarily').
- **Record:** S-61 reports Brooksby quoted his friend saying he knew the family through religious association, Robinson was in Washington County, and they were working to get him to come in voluntarily.
  - S-61 para 105 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "I know the family through religious association and he's in Washington County now, and we're working on trying to get him to come in voluntarily.'""

### C-975 (ep 11)
- **Claim:** In the Brooksby clip the host plays, Nate Brooksby says that within the hour his friend drove Tyler Robinson and his parents to his office, where Tyler was greeted by plain-clothes detectives.
- **Record:** Agent Davis testified Robinson came to the sheriff's office with his parents and a family friend (S-4 37:00), and the ruling found the family friend picked him up to take him there (S-7 07:55:06); the 'plain clothes detectives' detail is not addressed.
  - S-4 (37:00) (testimony, SBI Agent Brian Davis (witness)): "he'd come there with his parents and a family friend."
  - S-7 (07:55:06) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "At approximately 8:30 PM, a family friend went to pick up Mr. Robinson and take him to the sheriff's office. He asked Mr. Robinson if he had any property or clothes that he used during the incident. Mr. Robinson responded that he had discarded those items."

### C-976 (ep 11)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Brooksby says Tyler's arrival at his office was treated "as delicate and as soft as possible" because he was "fearful of a SWAT team hit" on his house or of being shot by law enforcement.
- **Record:** S-61 reports Brooksby said Robinson was truly fearful of being shot by law enforcement and of a SWAT team hitting his parents' house or apartment.
  - S-61 para 103 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "Robinson was "truly fearful of being shot by law enforcement," as well as fearful of a SWAT team hit on his house, Washington County Sheriff Nate Brooksby said Wednesday during a brief news conference."
  - S-61 para 109 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): ""He didn't want a big SWAT team hitting his parents' house or his apartment."

### C-977 (ep 11)
- **Claim:** In the same clip, Brooksby says Tyler knew it was inevitable he would be caught, given the law-enforcement pressure and his picture and the gun being in the news.
- **Record:** S-61 reports Brooksby said Robinson knew it was 'just inevitable' he would be caught after his pictures and the gun were in the news.
  - S-61 para 107 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "The sheriff said Robinson knew it was "just inevitable" that he was going to get caught after his pictures and the gun he allegedly used were reported in the news."

### C-995 (ep 11)
- **Claim:** In the clip the host plays, Brooksby says that at exactly 8:02 on the 11th he received a call from a friend who is a retired detective who worked for him for many years, and that court documents referenced the friend as a retired Washington County deputy.
- **Record:** S-61 reports Brooksby said a retired-detective friend who worked for him for many years called him on Sept. 11, and The Salt Lake Tribune (S-18) reports the family friend described as a retired deputy sheriff (the exact 8:02 time is not reported).
  - S-61 para 105 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): ""I received a call from a friend of mine who happens to be a retired detective that worked for me for many years," Brooksby said."
  - S-61 para 103 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "Brooksby also recounted how a friend and former deputy called him on Thursday"
  - S-18 para 41 (tier2_attributed, Robinson's parents): "The parents told police they asked their son to speak to a family friend, who is a retired deputy sheriff."

### C-998 (ep 11)
- **Claim:** The host says her team figured out who the retired friend is, names him as former Deputy Mike Mitchell, and says they have tried to get in touch with him.
- **Record:** Agent Davis testified the family friend was Mike Mitchell (S-4 37:56), and the Information describes the family friend as a retired deputy sheriff (S-1 p. 7).
  - S-4 (37:56) (testimony, SBI Agent Brian Davis (witness)): "Also, at that time, the family friend, Mike Mitchell, was interviewed by other agents as well."
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "convinced Robinson to speak with a family friend who is a retired deputy sheriff."

### C-1110 (ep 12)
- **Claim:** The host says George Zinn, the decoy boy that day, was later arrested because he had images of children being sexually abused on his phone.
- **Record:** KSL (S-30) reports Zinn was charged with sexual exploitation of a minor after, per the charges, investigators found inappropriate images of children on his phone.
  - S-30 para 12 (tier2_reported, KSL (photo caption)): "George Zinn, 71, who was arrested after Charlie Kirk was shot at Utah Valley University in Orem on Sept. 10, was charged this week with obstruction of justice and sexual exploitation of a minor."
  - S-30 para 30 (tier2_attributed, charging documents): "Investigators went through the phone and found multiple inappropriate images containing children in his sent folder, charges state."

### C-1117 (ep 12)
- **Claim:** The host says Charlie was set to do two events in Utah a mere 20 days apart, UVU and then USU, on campuses just under two hours apart by car.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report (S-12 p. 46) says the same TPUSA tour was scheduled at USU on September 30, 20 days after the UVU event; the driving distance is not addressed.
  - S-12 p. 46 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "arranged to observe the event because the same TPUSA tour was scheduled to occur at USU on September 30."

### C-1123 (ep 12)
- **Claim:** The host says that states typically require an ambulance on standby above a certain attendance, that she does not know Utah law, and that it seemed atypical to her that there was no ambulance on standby.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report (S-12 p. 59) finds no ambulance was staged on site for the September 10 event; the host's remarks about other states' rules are opinion.
  - S-12 p. 59 (report_finding, Utah Valley University After-Action Report (Project Team)): "No ambulance was staged on site for the September 10 TPUSA event."

### C-1161 (ep 13)
- **Claim:** The host says she was told that, despite public rumors to the contrary, an autopsy of Charlie Kirk was performed.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified an autopsy was conducted by the Medical Examiner's Office, by Dr. Guajardo (S-2 06:00:04, 06:00:30).
  - S-2 (06:00:04) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "An autopsy was conducted by the Medical Examiner's Office."
  - S-2 (06:00:30) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "Dr. Guajardo, I believe is how it's pronounced."

### C-1333 (ep 15)
- **Claim:** The host says Charlie Kirk was wearing a Saint Michael pendant when he died and says this and related points are indisputable facts.
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-26) reports Erika Kirk wore the St. Michael pendant her husband was wearing when he was shot.
  - S-26 para 43 (tier2_reported, New York Times): "Around her neck was the pendant of St. Michael that her husband was wearing when he was shot."

### C-1551 (ep 17)
- **Claim:** Wolicki stated the pre-assassination meeting was called by Charlie, was on the schedule for weeks, and was for the purpose of preparing Israel-related talking points for Charlie's new campus tour starting in Utah.
- **Record:** S-15 reports Rabbi Wolicki said Kirk initiated the meeting because he was starting a campus tour the next day and expected Israel questions, and The Salt Lake Tribune (S-45) reports UVU was the tour's first stop ('on the schedule for weeks' is not reported).
  - S-15 para 163 (tier2_attributed, Pesach Wolicki): ""The meeting was one he initiated," Wolicki said. "He was heading out on a campus tour the next day and knew he’d face many questions about Israel."
  - S-45 para 26 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "The UVU stop was meant to be the first on a new campus tour. Harpole said his team spent weeks preparing for the visit,"

### C-1611 (ep 18)
- **Claim:** Per Andrew Kolvet's tweet the host reads, the surgeon stated the bullet should have exited, that people behind Charlie could have been killed, and characterized the outcome as "an absolute miracle."
- **Record:** Fox News (S-28) reports Kolvet said Kirk's surgeon called it an 'absolute miracle' that the bullet did not exit his body (the people-behind detail is only in an unconfirmed relay).
  - S-28 para 156 (tier2_attributed, Andrew Kolvet): "Turning Point USA spokesman Andrew Kolvet on Saturday said Charlie Kirk 's surgeon called it an "absolute miracle" that the bullet that killed him didn’t exit his body"

### C-1612 (ep 18)
- **Claim:** The host says she is telling viewers 100% that Charlie Kirk was shot from the front and the bullet did not exit, and that she has fact-checked this from multiple sources.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7 07:46:29, 08:16:02) finds the bullet traveled front to back and did not exit Kirk's body.
  - S-7 (08:16:02) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "the bullet traveled anatomically from front to back and downward after striking Mr. Kirk. The projectile did not exit his body."
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The trajectory of the bullet was anatomically from front to back, slightly left to right and downward."

### C-1615 (ep 18)
- **Claim:** The host says that, as she has fact-checked with multiple sources, a bullet fragment was recovered from Charlie's neck, in line with the shoulder blade near the spine.
- **Record:** The ruling finds the bullet came to rest in the tissues of Kirk's upper back left of the midline (S-7 07:46:29), and Faumuina testified the bullet jacket fragment and other fragments were recovered from Kirk at autopsy (S-5 05:30:42).
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The bullet did not exit Mr. Kirk's body, but came to rest and tissues in his upper back left of the middle line."
  - S-5 (05:30:42) (testimony, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "Exhibit 6 is the bullet jacket fragment, as well as some other fragments that were recovered from Mr. Kirk at the time of autopsy."

### C-1661 (ep 18)
- **Claim:** The host says the bullet went straight into the front left lower side of Charlie's neck, essentially exploded his artery and did not exit the back, but that a bullet fragment was found there.
- **Record:** The ruling (S-7 07:46:29) finds a neck wound with a front-to-back, slightly left-to-right, downward path, no exit, and the bullet coming to rest in the upper back; the artery detail is not addressed.
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The trajectory of the bullet was anatomically from front to back, slightly left to right and downward."
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The bullet did not exit Mr. Kirk's body, but came to rest and tissues in his upper back left of the middle line."

### C-1930 (ep 20)
- **Claim:** The host alleges that Brigitte Macron was born Jean-Michel Trogneux, transitioned, and was put in a position of power; Brigitte Macron disputes this and is suing the host.
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-66) reports Brigitte Macron sued Owens for defamation over her claim that Macron is transgender and that the suit is pending in Delaware; this corroborates only that the host makes the allegation and Macron disputes it in court, not the allegation itself.
  - S-66 para 24 (tier2_reported, The New York Times): "In July, Owens was sued for defamation by Brigitte Macron, the wife of French President Emmanuel Macron, for claiming she is transgender."
  - S-66 para 24 (tier2_reported, The New York Times): "The lawsuit from Brigitte Macron is still pending in Delaware state court."

### C-2015 (ep 22)
- **Claim:** The host says FlightRadar data shows Egyptian Air Force plane SU-BTT arrived at Provo Airport on September 4, 2025 and departed Provo on September 10, 2025 at 7:14 a.m. local time, with an interim layover at Wilmington, Delaware before continuing to Cairo.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-13) shows SU-BTT (Falcon 7X, hex 0101d3) taking off from Provo at 13:14:03Z (07:14 MDT) on 2025-09-10 and arriving at Wilmington, Delaware at about 16:50Z; its 4 September arrival is outside this one-day archive.
  - S-13 hex 0101d3, 13:14:03Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "0101d3 SU-BTT FA7X: takeoff 13:14:03Z at 40.2208,-111.7241 (KPVU), gs 144.5 kt; last point 16:50:37Z at 39.663,-75.605 (KILG)"

### C-2059 (ep 22)
- **Claim:** The host says the second plane in the viewer's comment is the LSA LLC plane N1098L, which flew up to Montana and turned around, and says she has not back-checked the commenter's claim that Israeli defense companies use it.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-13) shows N1098L flying north from New Mexico via a low pass over Provo (09:16 MDT) to Kalispell, Montana (about 10:34 MDT), and turning south again at 11:27 MDT; the operator name and the commenter's other claims are not in this record.
  - S-13 hex a0299e, 13:59Z-17:27Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a0299e N1098L: 13:59:07Z at 32.680,-107.135; 15:16:53Z low point 4750 ft near KPVU; 16:34:32Z at 48.295,-114.272 (KGPI); 17:27:16Z departs 48.310,-114.256 southbound"

### C-2060 (ep 22)
- **Claim:** Mark says a screenshot shows N1098L was in Idaho at the moment Charlie was shot; the host adds that it was about 20 minutes out and did not get over Provo until about 20 minutes after he was shot.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-13) puts N1098L (Global 6000, hex a0299e) over southeast Idaho at 12:23:24 MDT, 224 km from UVU, at 29,650 ft; it descended over Provo at about 12:45 to 12:50 MDT (down to about 5,075 ft) and climbed out east.
  - S-13 hex a0299e, 18:23:24Z and 18:45-18:50Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a0299e N1098L GLEX: 18:23:24Z at 42.222,-112.407, 29650 ft (224 km from UVU); 18:48:29Z at 40.255,-111.755, 5075 ft (4 km from UVU)"

### C-2144 (ep 23)
- **Claim:** The host says N102DZ (G5 private jet) departed Scottsdale for Salt Lake City at 7:12 AM local time on the day of Charlie Kirk's assassination and landed at 9:23 AM local.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-13) shows N102DZ (Gulfstream V, hex a00c85) taking off from Scottsdale at 14:12:34Z (07:12 MST) and its last point on landing at Salt Lake City at 15:23:38Z (09:23 MDT).
  - S-13 hex a00c85, 14:12:34Z-15:23:38Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a00c85 N102DZ GLF5: takeoff 14:12:34Z at KSDL; landing ~15:23:38Z at KSLC 40.785,-111.962"

### C-2145 (ep 23)
- **Claim:** The host says N102DZ left Salt Lake City and headed to Scottsdale at about 1:00 PM local on September 10, 2025, was on the ground in Scottsdale for only about 15 minutes, and then turned around and flew out again.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-13) shows N102DZ taking off from Salt Lake City at 19:00:43Z (13:00 MDT), landing at Scottsdale at 20:11:55Z and taking off again at 20:31:57Z, about 20 minutes on the ground.
  - S-13 hex a00c85, 19:00:43Z-20:31:57Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a00c85 N102DZ: takeoff 19:00:43Z KSLC; landing 20:11:55Z KSDL; takeoff 20:31:57Z KSDL"

### C-2287 (ep 25)
- **Claim:** The host says that because UVU will not release the security plan, she is pretty sure the event was an extraordinary first-time situation in which there was no security plan.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report (S-12 pp. 13, 17) finds the event was not supported by a written Security Assessment or Event Action Plan, though UVU took basic planning steps; the 'first time' speculation is not addressed.
  - S-12 p. 13 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "the club event was not supported by a comprehensive written Security Assessment or Event Action Plan"
  - S-12 p. 17 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "UVU took basic steps to plan for the September 10 TPUSA event, but the event was not supported by a written Event Action Plan"

### C-2447 (ep 27)
- **Claim:** Allie Beth Stuckey tweets that the UVU campus stop was "requested in July" rather than "approved in July," and that Charlie texted her at the end of August about a November campus event approximately 2 months out.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report shows TPUSA's initial request to UVU was a July 10 email (S-12 p. 48) and that MEAC formally approved the event only on August 27 (S-12 p. 19), i.e. requested, not approved, in July.
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "the initial e-mail request from TPUSA to UVU on July 10, 2025, also specifically requested that the event be held at the Fountain Courtyard."
  - S-12 p. 19 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "August 27 MEAC formally approved the event. TPUSA requested event support items including barriers, tables, chairs, power, and green room access."

### C-2668 (ep 30)
- **Claim:** According to local Utah news cited by the host, the UVU Turning Point chapter requested Charlie Kirk as a speaker on July 18.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report timeline (S-12 p. 18) records that on July 18 the UVU TPUSA Club submitted a formal event request for a Charlie Kirk appearance.
  - S-12 p. 18 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "July 18 Formal event request submitted by UVU TPUSA Club. Event described as an appearance by Charlie Kirk to discuss current events. External ticketing by TPUSA anticipated."

### C-2669 (ep 30)
- **Claim:** The host says she learned that UVU communicated to Turning Point USA that the outdoor amphitheater was not advisable for safety reasons, and that Turning Point USA nonetheless insisted on the outdoor venue.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report (S-12 p. 48) finds UVU expressed concerns about the outdoor location, including at the August 25 MEAC meeting, but agreed when TPUSA's Maycee Crofts insisted; p. 13 calls it 'the outdoor location insisted upon by TPUSA'.
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "multiple interviews the Project Team conducted indicated that UVU had expressed concerns about the outdoor location, including during the August 25 MEAC meeting, but ultimately agreed to TPUSA’s requested venue location when TPUSA representative Maycee Crofts insisted on it."
  - S-12 p. 13 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "The outdoor location insisted upon by TPUSA for their event created significant protective operations considerations."

### C-2672 (ep 30)
- **Claim:** The host says UVU showed Turning Point multiple options both inside and outside and then recommended against holding the event in the amphitheater, citing that it could not control traffic flow.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12 p. 64) finds UVU raised concerns about the controllability of TPUSA's requested outdoor location and suggested an alternative where access could be better managed, and (p. 18) that the event ultimately proceeded in the requested location.
  - S-12 p. 64 (report_finding, Utah Valley University After-Action Report (Project Team)): "UVU participants raised concerns during the MEAC process regarding the controllability of the requested outdoor location and suggested an alternative location where access could be more effectively managed."
  - S-12 p. 18 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "the committee ultimately allowed the event to proceed in the requested location and allowed TPUSA to manage ticketing."

### C-2937 (ep 33)
- **Claim:** Host reports that tail N560TW departed Scottsdale, Arizona on September 10 and landed at Provo at 10:06 a.m., then departed for Santa Barbara and later returned to Scottsdale by 12:44 p.m.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-13) shows N560TW (Citation XLS, hex a728a8) landing at Provo at 16:07:13Z (10:07 MDT), leaving at 16:27:04Z (10:27 MDT) for Santa Barbara, and landing back at Scottsdale at 19:43:16Z (12:43 MST).
  - S-13 hex a728a8, 15:00:55Z-19:43:16Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a728a8 N560TW C56X: takeoff 15:00:55Z KSDL; landing 16:07:13Z KPVU; takeoff 16:27:04Z KPVU; landing 18:11:49Z KSBA; takeoff 18:37:30Z KSBA; landing 19:43:16Z KSDL"

### C-3011 (ep 34)
- **Claim:** The host describes Brian Harpole as one of Charlie Kirk's long-term security team members, who on the day was standing front right of Charlie with a bag across him and ran over to him, and says his interview was with Shawn Ryan.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report (S-12 p. 47) identifies Brian Harpole as head of the security team working for TPUSA on September 10 and records his Shawn Ryan Podcast appearance (SRS #254); 'long-term' and his position on the day are not addressed.
  - S-12 p. 47 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Although the Project Team did not receive a response to its requests to interview Brian Harpole, head of the Integrity Solutions security team working for TPUSA on September 10"
  - S-12 p. 47 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "appearance on the Shawn Ryan Podcast, titled “Brian Harpole – Groundbreaking Evidence From Charlie Kirk’s Head of Security / SRS #254."

### C-3014 (ep 34)
- **Claim:** In the Shawn Ryan interview clip the host plays, Brian Harpole says, for edification, that the Sorensen Center was the building in front of the Losee Center.
- **Record:** Bagley testified the Sorensen building sits between the courtyard and the Losee building, which is east of it (S-2 53:02-53:12).
  - S-2 (53:02) (testimony, Officer Christopher Bagley, Spanish Fork PD, formerly UVU PD (witness; Rev mislabels him 'Richard Novak')): "It doesn't run right up against it. So the Sorensen building is kind of like right between those two."
  - S-2 (53:12) (testimony, Officer Christopher Bagley, Spanish Fork PD, formerly UVU PD (witness; Rev mislabels him 'Richard Novak')): "East."

### C-3018 (ep 34)
- **Claim:** In the interview, Harpole says he spent thousands of dollars on drones last year and got a pilot licensed, but that he could not fly in the Provo airspace under the rules and there were secondary restrictions likely tied to heavy foot traffic.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report (S-12 p. 48) records Harpole stating his team had drones but did not use them because of FAA restrictions tied to Provo Airport airspace; the cost and pilot licence details are not addressed.
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Harpole also stated that although his team had drones, they did not use them during the event because of FAA restrictions related to Provo Airport airspace."

### C-3019 (ep 34)
- **Claim:** In the interview, Harpole says the school and its police department could have flown drones but did not have them, while the Orem police department has an active, professional drone unit.
- **Record:** Bagley testified UVU Police had no drone capability (S-2 02:54:15), and the After-Action Report finds the Orem Police Department maintains drone capability (S-12 p. 77).
  - S-2 (02:54:15) (testimony, Officer Bagley, UVU Police (witness)): "No."
  - S-12 p. 77 (report_finding, Utah Valley University After-Action Report (Project Team)): "the Orem Police Department maintains drone capability that could support situational awareness during large outdoor events"

### C-3020 (ep 34)
- **Claim:** In the interview, Harpole says he asked Orem police about an MOU or mutual aid agreement with the school and was told yes, then asked whether the school called them for assistance.
- **Record:** The Salt Lake Tribune (S-45) reports Harpole said Orem police have a memorandum with UVU to provide backup and Orem told him it was not asked to help.
  - S-45 para 47 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "Orem’s police department has a memorandum with UVU to provide backup when needed,"

### C-3106 (ep 35)
- **Claim:** The host says Brian Harpole, in his Shawn Ryan interview, basically said TPUSA did not have drones at the event because he "wasn't allowed to have drones."
- **Record:** The After-Action Report (S-12 pp. 24, 48) records Harpole saying in his podcast interview that his team did not use drones because of FAA restrictions on Provo Airport airspace.
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Harpole also stated that although his team had drones, they did not use them during the event because of FAA restrictions related to Provo Airport airspace."
  - S-12 p. 24 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "Brian Harpole discussed the September 10 TPUSA event in a public podcast interview, including event planning, venue concerns, elevated positions, drone use, and coordination with UVU officials."

### C-3112 (ep 35)
- **Claim:** In the clip, Frank Turek says he told Charlie he did not like the place because there were too many buildings, and that Charlie shrugged it off.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-27) reports Frank Turek said he told Kirk he was concerned about the large number of nearby buildings and the safety risk they posed.
  - S-27 para 163 (tier2_attributed, Frank Turek): "Turek told Kirk he was concerned about the large number of nearby buildings and the safety risk they could pose"

### C-3191 (ep 36)
- **Claim:** The host says that in early August Turning Point was scouting the UVU location, the school told them it was not a great place because there was no traffic control, and Turning Point and headquarters employees insisted it was the best location, which was Charlie Kirk's preferred one.
- **Record:** The After-Action Report (S-12 pp. 41, 48, 64) finds UVU raised concerns about controlling the outdoor location and suggested an alternative, but TPUSA's Maycee Crofts insisted on the Fountain Courtyard because Kirk had spoken there before; the 'early August' timing is not confirmed (the concerns are dated to the August 25 MEAC meeting).
  - S-12 p. 64 (report_finding, Utah Valley University After-Action Report (Project Team)): "UVU participants raised concerns during the MEAC process regarding the controllability of the requested outdoor location and suggested an alternative location where access could be more effectively managed."
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "multiple interviews the Project Team conducted indicated that UVU had expressed concerns about the outdoor location, including during the August 25 MEAC meeting, but ultimately agreed to TPUSA’s requested venue location when TPUSA representative Maycee Crofts insisted on it."
  - S-12 p. 41 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Crofts insisted on the use of the Fountain Courtyard location because Charlie Kirk had previously spoken on campus and TPUSA was familiar with that location."

### C-3215 (ep 36)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Brian Harpole presents what the host says is a text exchange between Dan Flood and Chief Long about student roof access (Sorensen Center staircases to walkways/roofs near the CK setup), asking for either controlled access or one of his guys to be there, with Chief Long's last reply "I got you covered."
- **Record:** The After-Action Report exhibit (S-12 p. 43) shows Flood's September 8 texts to Chief Long about student roof access by the Sorensen staircases near the CK setup, asking for controlled access or one of his guys, and Long's reply 'I got you covered'.
  - S-12 p. 43 (exhibit, Dan Flood, TPUSA Security Supervisor (text to Chief Long, Sept 8, 2025)): "there is student roof access pretty close to where CK will be set up at Utah Valley (The Sorensen Student center has a couple stair cases that go up to the walkways on the roof)"
  - S-12 p. 43 (exhibit, Dan Flood, TPUSA Security Supervisor (text to Chief Long, Sept 8, 2025)): "I was told students have access above us, if this is true, it would be nice to either have it controlled access or allow one of my guys to be there as well."
  - S-12 p. 43 (exhibit, Chief Long (text to Dan Flood, Sept 8, 2025)): "Chief Long: I got you covered"

### C-3216 (ep 36)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Brian Harpole says the Sorensen Center was the building in front of the Losee Center (transcribed Sorenson and Lucy).
- **Record:** Bagley testified the Sorensen building sits between the courtyard and the Losee building, which is east of it (S-2 53:02-53:12).
  - S-2 (53:02) (testimony, Officer Christopher Bagley, Spanish Fork PD, formerly UVU PD (witness; Rev mislabels him 'Richard Novak')): "It doesn't run right up against it. So the Sorensen building is kind of like right between those two."
  - S-2 (53:12) (testimony, Officer Christopher Bagley, Spanish Fork PD, formerly UVU PD (witness; Rev mislabels him 'Richard Novak')): "East."

### C-3227 (ep 36)
- **Claim:** Washington County Sheriff Nate Brooksby, in his September 17, 2025 press conference as reported by KUTV, stated: "Within the hour, my friend drove Tyler and his parents to my office where he was greeted by plain clothes detectives."
- **Record:** Agent Davis testified Robinson came to the sheriff's office with his parents and a family friend (S-4 37:00), and Hull agreed he was driven there with his parents rather than arrested (S-3 02:10:57); the detectives detail is not addressed.
  - S-4 (37:00) (testimony, SBI Agent Brian Davis (witness)): "he'd come there with his parents and a family friend."
  - S-3 (02:10:57) (testimony, Agent Hull, former Utah State Bureau of Investigation agent (witness; Rev also renders Holt/Hall)): "That's my understanding, yes."

### C-3230 (ep 36)
- **Claim:** The KUTV report states that the alleged shooter was taken into custody in Washington County less than two days after the shooting.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7, 07:56:00) finds Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office about 9:00 p.m. on September 11, 2025, and Agent Davis (S-4, 45:58) testified his phone was seized from his person there.
  - S-7 (07:56:00) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "At approximately 9:00 PM, Mr. Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office. He was wearing a maroon shirt and jeans."
  - S-4 (45:58) (testimony, SBI Agent Brian Davis (witness)): "his cell phone was seized and taken from his person, but that was the only thing that was taken from him at that time."

### C-3451 (ep 39)
- **Claim:** The host says that in early August Turning Point scouted the location and requested that Charlie speak in the amphitheater, that insiders said he loved an amphitheater event in Nashville, and that TPUSA insisted, with Maycee Crofts saying this is what Charlie wants.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, pp. 41, 48) finds UVU agreed to TPUSA's requested Fountain Courtyard venue when TPUSA representative Maycee Crofts insisted on it.
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "multiple interviews the Project Team conducted indicated that UVU had expressed concerns about the outdoor location, including during the August 25 MEAC meeting, but ultimately agreed to TPUSA’s requested venue location when TPUSA representative Maycee Crofts insisted on it."
  - S-12 p. 41 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Crofts insisted on the use of the Fountain Courtyard location because Charlie Kirk had previously spoken on campus and TPUSA was familiar with that location."

### C-3511 (ep 40)
- **Claim:** The host says Blake Neff is the producer and a rotating on-air talent of the Charlie Kirk Show, and that she expected TPUSA spokesperson Andrew Kolvet to deliver the response.
- **Record:** S-49 identifies Blake Neff as a producer on The Charlie Kirk Show (the 'rotating on-air talent' detail and the host's expectation about Kolvet are not assessed).
  - S-49 para 103 (tier2_attributed, Blake Neff): "Blake Neff, a producer on "The Charlie Kirk Show" podcast disputed "claims of financial impropriety and fraud at Turning Point adding up into the millions of dollars"

### C-3537 (ep 40)
- **Claim:** In the statement the host plays, Blake Neff says Charlie made sure TPUSA was audited by a third party every year.
- **Record:** S-49 quotes Neff: 'Charlie made sure the organization was audited by a third party every year.'
  - S-49 para 104 (tier2_attributed, Blake Neff): ""Charlie made sure the organization was audited by a third party every year," Neff said."

### C-3538 (ep 40)
- **Claim:** In the video statement, TPUSA asserted that Charlie personally reviewed and signed off on every expense report and every bill paid by the organization down to a single United States dollar.
- **Record:** CBS News (S-49) reports TPUSA's Blake Neff saying Charlie Kirk personally signed off on every expense; the 'down to a single dollar' wording is not quoted.
  - S-49 para 104 (tier2_attributed, Blake Neff): ""Charlie made sure the organization was audited by a third party every year," Neff said."

### C-3539 (ep 40)
- **Claim:** In the video statement, TPUSA asserted that the organization has never missed a 990 filing deadline.
- **Record:** CBS News (S-49) quotes Blake Neff for TPUSA: 'We have never missed a 990 deadline.'
  - S-49 para 104 (tier2_attributed, Blake Neff): "We have never missed a 990 deadline."

### C-3620 (ep 41)
- **Claim:** The host says the plane she labels blue, SU-BND, was transponding on the ground at Duncan Aviation on the day of Charlie's assassination but did not go anywhere.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-13) shows SU-BND (Gulfstream IV, hex 01003e) transmitting on the ground at Provo Airport from 10:05 to 11:34 MDT and 13:40 to 14:29 MDT on 2025-09-10, with no airborne points that day (data runs to 18:00 MDT).
  - S-13 hex 01003e, 16:05:35Z-20:29:38Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "01003e SU-BND GLF4: 257 points 16:05:35Z-20:29:38Z, all on ground at 40.2300,-111.7280 (KPVU); no airborne points"

### C-3700 (ep 42)
- **Claim:** The host says Tyler Robinson, the alleged shooter, notoriously was wearing a maroon shirt at the UVU event.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7, 07:49:47) finds Robinson walked toward the amphitheater wearing a maroon short-sleeve shirt, and Agent Hull (S-3, 00:47:48) testified to a maroon T-shirt.
  - S-7 (07:49:47) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "A person identified as Mr. Robinson exited the Charger and walked on foot out of the parking garage toward the outdoor amphitheater on campus. He was wearing a maroon short sleeve shirt, shorts, a hat, and a backpack."
  - S-3 (00:47:48) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "It's a maroon color T-shirt with gray or olive colored shorts and then converse shoes."

### C-3732 (ep 42)
- **Claim:** The host reads a commenter who says Bari Weiss is interviewing Erika Kirk on December 13.
- **Record:** CBS News (S-49) reports Erika Kirk would be interviewed in a town hall moderated by Bari Weiss broadcast Dec. 13, 2025.
  - S-49 para 111 (tier2_reported, CBS News): "Kirk is going to be interviewed this week in a one-hour town hall event moderated by Bari Weiss, CBS News' editor-in-chief, that will be broadcast on Saturday, Dec. 13"

### C-3733 (ep 42)
- **Claim:** The host says she saw some news about Bari Weiss sitting down with Erika Kirk, though she is unsure whether it is a New York Times or CBS event.
- **Record:** CBS News (S-49) reports Bari Weiss would interview Erika Kirk in a CBS News town hall (a CBS event).
  - S-49 para 111 (tier2_reported, CBS News): "Kirk is going to be interviewed this week in a one-hour town hall event moderated by Bari Weiss, CBS News' editor-in-chief, that will be broadcast on Saturday, Dec. 13"

### C-3800 (ep 44)
- **Claim:** The host says that in the final days before the assassination Charlie Kirk made a first-time, quick trip to Asia, going first to Korea for a couple of days and then to Japan.
- **Record:** NBC News (S-50) reports Kirk spoke in Seoul on Sept. 6 and then left for Tokyo less than a week before he was killed; 'first-time' is not verified.
  - S-50 para 38 (tier2_reported, NBC News): "Less than a week before he was killed, Charlie Kirk was in Asia spreading his conservative and anti-immigration message, making stops in South Korea and Japan"
  - S-50 para 33 (tier2_reported, NBC News): "Charlie Kirk speaking at the Build Up Korea conference in Seoul on Sept. 6."
  - S-50 para 90 (tier2_reported, NBC News): "After South Korea, Kirk left for Tokyo"

### C-3855 (ep 44)
- **Claim:** The host says Kirk was in Korea doing an event on September 6 and was in Japan on September 7, speaking on CNN Japan.
- **Record:** NBC News (S-50) shows Kirk at Build Up Korea in Seoul on Sept. 6 and reports he then went to Japan and gave CNN an interview there; the exact Sept. 7 date is not stated.
  - S-50 para 33 (tier2_reported, NBC News): "Charlie Kirk speaking at the Build Up Korea conference in Seoul on Sept. 6."
  - S-50 para 38 (tier2_reported, NBC News): "Less than a week before he was killed, Charlie Kirk was in Asia spreading his conservative and anti-immigration message, making stops in South Korea and Japan"
  - S-50 para 95 (tier2_attributed, Charlie Kirk): "telling CNN in an interview there that if the country were to receive a large influx of foreigners, “Japan’s not Japan anymore.”"

### C-3901 (ep 45)
- **Claim:** The host says Erika Kirk gave a live interview with Harris Faulkner on Fox News in which she addressed what the host calls the "conspiracy theories."
- **Record:** Fox News (S-36) reports Erika Kirk, speaking with Harris Faulkner on Dec. 10, 2025, fired back at conspiracy theorists.
  - S-36 para 9 (tier2_reported, Fox News): "Erika Kirk, the widow of Turning Point USA founder Charlie Kirk , fired back at conspiracy theorists"
  - S-36 para 15 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "telling Fox News’ Harris Faulkner she doesn’t have the time to "address the noise.""

### C-3904 (ep 45)
- **Claim:** In a Harris Faulkner interview clip the host plays, Erika Kirk asks that her family be allowed to keep private where Charlie Kirk is laid to rest, saying she wants one thing kept sacred to her family, in-laws, children and parents, and says a "museum style" memorial will be built at Turning Point USA.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-36) quotes Erika Kirk on 'Outnumbered' asking 'Can I have one thing?' about her husband's grave and saying a memorial will be built at TPUSA; 'museum style' is not quoted.
  - S-36 para 10 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): ""Can I have one thing? Can my children have one thing?" Kirk said on Wednesday’s " Outnumbered .""
  - S-36 para 11 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "We will be building the most beautiful memorial for my husband at Turning Point USA."
  - S-36 para 11 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "where I don't have to be worried about some secular revolutionary coming and destroying my husband's grave"

### C-3905 (ep 45)
- **Claim:** In the Fox clip Erika Kirk says she does not want to worry about "some secular revolutionary" coming to destroy her husband's grave while her daughter prays there.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-36) quotes Erika Kirk on not having to worry about 'some secular revolutionary coming and destroying my husband's grave.'
  - S-36 para 11 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "where I don't have to be worried about some secular revolutionary coming and destroying my husband's grave"

### C-3908 (ep 45)
- **Claim:** In the Fox clip Erika Kirk says that whenever she and TPUSA hear a lead or anything, they send it to the authorities.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-36) quotes Erika Kirk: 'Any time we hear a lead or anytime we hear anything, we send it to the authorities.'
  - S-36 para 17 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "Any time we hear a lead or anytime we hear anything, we send it to the authorities."

### C-3909 (ep 45)
- **Claim:** In the Fox clip Erika Kirk says people going after those she loves are making hundreds and thousands of dollars every single episode.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-36) quotes Erika Kirk: 'you're making hundreds and thousands of dollars every single episode going after the people that I love.'
  - S-36 para 21 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "you're making hundreds and thousands of dollars every single episode going after the people that I love"

### C-4002 (ep 46)
- **Claim:** The host says officials claimed Tyler Robinson was suicidal and also turned himself in because he was afraid of being shot by police, and asks how both can be true.
- **Record:** A Utah sheriff said Robinson feared being shot by police (S-61), and charging documents say he told his parents he couldn't go to jail and wanted to end it (S-33), so officials made both statements.
  - S-61 para 102 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "was afraid of being shot by police and ultimately agreed to a peaceful surrender with the help of his family, a sheriff in Utah said."
  - S-33 para 31 (tier2_attributed, charging documents): "Robinson implied that he was the shooter and stated that he couldn't go to jail and just wanted to end it"

### C-4080 (ep 47)
- **Claim:** The host says Andrew Kolvet posted a tweet saying he spoke with the surgeon, and reads a quote she calls allegedly from the surgeon: Charlie Kirk's bone was so healthy and dense that he was "like the man of steel" and the bullet should have gone through and through.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-28) reports Andrew Kolvet said he spoke with Kirk's surgeon about the bullet not exiting; the 'man of steel' wording appears only as Kolvet's unconfirmed relay, which matches the host's 'allegedly'.
  - S-28 para 156 (tier2_attributed, Andrew Kolvet): "Turning Point USA spokesman Andrew Kolvet on Saturday said Charlie Kirk 's surgeon called it an "absolute miracle" that the bullet that killed him didn’t exit his body"

### C-4091 (ep 47)
- **Claim:** The host says a lawyer involved in the case told her the probable cause hearing, at which evidence will first be presented to a judge, is scheduled for May (recorded as May 2026).
- **Record:** KSL (S-38), KUTV (S-55) and Deseret News (S-19) report Robinson's preliminary hearing was scheduled for May 18, 19 and 21, 2026.
  - S-38 para 18 (tier2_reported, KSL): "A preliminary hearing is currently scheduled for May 18, 19 and 21."
  - S-55 para 37 (tier2_reported, KUTV): "A preliminary hearing for Robinson was scheduled in May 2026. However, his attorneys have requested a continuance for time to go over discovery in the case."
  - S-19 para 58 (tier2_reported, Deseret News): "His next court date is scheduled for April 17, but the preliminary hearing is currently set for May 18, 19 and 21"

### C-4142 (ep 47)
- **Claim:** The host says she accurately said the Brian Harpole message that was shown belonged to Dan Flood, and that they did not do a walk-through of the venue that day.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, p. 44) finds there was no formal pre-event briefing or coordinated walk-through between UVUPD and Harpole, Flood and the TPUSA security team; who the message belonged to is not covered.
  - S-12 p. 44 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "The absence of a formal pre-event briefing and coordinated walk-through with Brian Harpole, Dan Flood, and the TPUSA security team was a critical missed opportunity."

### C-4145 (ep 47)
- **Claim:** A speaker on the Charlie Kirk Show reads from the charging document that Robinson's mother said Robinson had become more political over the last year, leaning left and becoming more pro-gay and trans rights oriented, and had begun dating his roommate.
- **Record:** The charging Information (S-1, p. 7) states Robinson's mother said he had become more political, left-leaning and pro-gay and trans-rights oriented, and had begun dating his roommate.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson had become more political and had started to lean more to the left – becoming more pro-gay and trans-rights oriented."
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson began to date his roommate, a biological male who was transitioning genders."

### C-4146 (ep 47)
- **Claim:** The Charlie Kirk Show reading of the charging document says that in one conversation before the shooting, Robinson said Charlie Kirk would hold an event at UVU, called it a stupid venue, and accused Kirk of spreading hate.
- **Record:** The charging Information (S-1, p. 7) states Robinson told family Kirk would hold an event at UVU, called it a "stupid venue" and accused Kirk of spreading hate.
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson mentioned that Charlie Kir k would be holding an event at UVU, which Robinson said was a “stupid venue” for the event. Robinson accused Kirk of spreading hate."

### C-4213 (ep 48)
- **Claim:** Frank Turek states on a podcast that Charlie's necklace, bearing a cross and medallions, was draped over his computer bag inside the transport vehicle, and that when a cop said they had been looking for it, he said he had it and took it.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-27) reports Frank Turek said Kirk's missing cross necklace was found draped over Turek's computer bag from the drive to the hospital; the police-officer exchange is not covered.
  - S-27 para 185 (tier2_attributed, Frank Turek): "Turek found the cross draped over his own computer bag, which Kirk was lying on as the team drove him to the hospital."
  - S-27 para 184 (tier2_attributed, Frank Turek): "Turek said Erika asked for her husband's cross necklace, which went missing during the rush."

### C-4238 (ep 48)
- **Claim:** Brian Harpole states in an interview that the hard conversations, information sharing and conference calls for the UVU event began on August 24, though the intelligence gathering started before that.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, pp. 18, 47) records Harpole saying in his November 17, 2025 podcast that his first meeting on the September 10 event was on August 24.
  - S-12 p. 47 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Harpole stated that his first meeting to discuss the September 10 TPUSA event occurred on August 24."
  - S-12 p. 18 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "Brian Harpole stated during a November 17, 2025 public podcast interview that his first meeting to discuss the September 10 TPUSA event occurred on this date, one day before the MEAC meeting."

### C-4239 (ep 48)
- **Claim:** In the clip, Brian Harpole says the process started before the 24th with intelligence gathering, while the hard conversations, information sharing, conference calls and data sheets started on the 24th of the prior month.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12 pp. 18, 47) records that Harpole stated in his November 17, 2025 podcast that his first meeting to discuss the September 10 event occurred on August 24.
  - S-12 p. 47 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Harpole stated that his first meeting to discuss the September 10 TPUSA event occurred on August 24."
  - S-12 p. 18 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "Brian Harpole stated during a November 17, 2025 public podcast interview that his first meeting to discuss the September 10 TPUSA event occurred on this date, one day before the MEAC meeting."

### C-4268 (ep 48)
- **Claim:** Answering a commenter, the host says the meeting she described the previous day was a four and a half hour meeting and she had an hour to relate what was said.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-24) quotes Owens describing an 'extremely productive 4 1/2 hour meeting' with Erika Kirk.
  - S-24 para 164 (tier2_attributed, Candace Owens): ""Erika and I had an extremely productive 4 1/2 hour meeting that I think we both feel should have taken place a lot earlier than it did," Owens said."

### C-4725 (ep 54)
- **Claim:** In a clip of the Shawn Ryan interview the host plays, Brian Harpole says that for that Charlie Kirk event the hard conversations and information sharing started on the 24th of the month prior, with intelligence gathering beginning before that.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, p. 47) records that on the Shawn Ryan Podcast (SRS #254) Harpole said his first meeting on the September 10 event was on August 24.
  - S-12 p. 47 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "appearance on the Shawn Ryan Podcast, titled “Brian Harpole – Groundbreaking Evidence From Charlie Kirk’s Head of Security / SRS #254."
  - S-12 p. 47 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Harpole stated that his first meeting to discuss the September 10 TPUSA event occurred on August 24."

### C-4726 (ep 54)
- **Claim:** The host says that elsewhere in the interview Harpole describes a conversation with the chief of police and is upset the chief was not covering the rooftops, because he feels his team was on it.
- **Record:** The Salt Lake Tribune (S-45) reports Harpole texted UVU Chief Jeff Long asking for officers on the rooftops or to assign one of his own men; that he was upset is the host's characterisation.
  - S-45 para 10 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "Two days before Charlie Kirk was set to speak at Utah Valley University , the head of his contracted security team texted the school’s police chief."
  - S-45 para 12 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "He asked UVU police Chief Jeff Long if the school could have officers patrol that — or allow Harpole to assign “one of my guys to be there as well.”"

### C-4727 (ep 54)
- **Claim:** The host says Harpole's account involved coordinating with whole police departments and discussing drones, and that drones were not allowed so they had to let that go.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12 pp. 24, 48) records Harpole's podcast account covering drone use and coordination with UVU, including that his team had drones but did not use them because of FAA restrictions near Provo Airport.
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "Harpole also stated that although his team had drones, they did not use them during the event because of FAA restrictions related to Provo Airport airspace."
  - S-12 p. 24 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "Brian Harpole discussed the September 10 TPUSA event in a public podcast interview, including event planning, venue concerns, elevated positions, drone use, and coordination with UVU officials."

### C-4757 (ep 54)
- **Claim:** The host says George Zinn was arrested and it was then determined he had pornographic images of children on his phone, and she refers to him as the decoy.
- **Record:** KSL (S-30) reports George Zinn, arrested after the shooting, was charged with sexual exploitation of a minor after investigators found images of children on his phone; 'decoy' is the host's label.
  - S-30 para 12 (tier2_reported, KSL (photo caption)): "George Zinn, 71, who was arrested after Charlie Kirk was shot at Utah Valley University in Orem on Sept. 10, was charged this week with obstruction of justice and sexual exploitation of a minor."
  - S-30 para 30 (tier2_attributed, charging documents): "Investigators went through the phone and found multiple inappropriate images containing children in his sent folder, charges state."

### C-4829 (ep 54)
- **Claim:** The host says that after Charlie Kirk went to Korea and met a pastor who was then arrested, he went to Japan, and she asks what Kirk had to do with Korea.
- **Record:** NBC News (S-50) reports Kirk visited South Korea and then left for Tokyo; the pastor-arrest point is not covered.
  - S-50 para 38 (tier2_reported, NBC News): "Less than a week before he was killed, Charlie Kirk was in Asia spreading his conservative and anti-immigration message, making stops in South Korea and Japan"
  - S-50 para 90 (tier2_reported, NBC News): "After South Korea, Kirk left for Tokyo"

### C-5208 (ep 58)
- **Claim:** The host says Astrid Tuminez has just stepped down from Utah Valley University as its president, as just announced.
- **Record:** KSL (S-60) refers to Astrid Tuminez as former UVU president; the timing of the announcement is not established.
  - S-60 para 18 (tier2_reported, KSL): "against UVU, the UVU Police Department, police Chief Jeffrey Long, former UVU President Astrid Tuminez and the state of Utah."

### C-5209 (ep 58)
- **Claim:** The host says Tuminez was not at UVU on the day of Charlie's assassination despite it being the first week of school.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report timeline (S-12, p. 23) records that at 1:55 p.m. on September 10 President Tuminez was returning from Atlanta.
  - S-12 p. 23 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "1:55 p.m. President Tuminez advised that she was returning from Atlanta and directed VP Val Peterson to manage University communications."

### C-5431 (ep 61)
- **Claim:** The host says she received a cease and desist letter from Turning Point USA, her first legal letter of 2026, when it was 20 days into January.
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-66) reports Turning Point USA sent Owens a cease and desist letter in January 2026, portions of which she read on her show.
  - S-66 para 28 (tier2_reported, The New York Times): "In January, Turning Point also sent Owens a cease and desist letter, portions of which she read on her show at the time."

### C-5460 (ep 61)
- **Claim:** The TPUSA letter demands that Owens cease stating or implying that TPUSA or its personnel played any part in, knew about beforehand, participated in the day of, or covered up the truth about the Kirk assassination.
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-66) quotes the TPUSA letter demanding Owens stop claiming it 'knew about the assassination beforehand, participated in the assassination day-of, or covered up the truth.'
  - S-66 para 28 (tier2_attributed, Turning Point USA (cease and desist letter)): "“knew about the assassination beforehand, participated in the assassination day-of, or covered up the truth about the assassination after the fact.”"
  - S-66 para 28 (tier2_reported, The New York Times): "In January, Turning Point also sent Owens a cease and desist letter, portions of which she read on her show at the time."

### C-5609 (ep 63)
- **Claim:** The episode presents that Erika Kirk gave an in-person interview to the New York Times at her and Charlie's condo on September 18, 2025 – eight days after Charlie's public assassination.
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-26) reports Erika Kirk gave an hour-and-a-half interview on Thursday, eight days after Kirk was killed; the condo location is not covered.
  - S-26 para 34 (tier2_reported, New York Times): "she recalled during an hour and a half interview Thursday, eight days after Charlie Kirk, 31,"

### C-5612 (ep 63)
- **Claim:** The host reads that the New York Times describes Erika Kirk as now left to care for a three-year-old daughter and a one-year-old son.
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-26) describes Erika Kirk as 'now left to care for a 3-year-old daughter and a 1-year-old son.'
  - S-26 para 50 (tier2_reported, New York Times): "Erika Kirk, now left to care for a 3-year-old daughter and a 1-year-old son."

### C-5614 (ep 63)
- **Claim:** The host says the public memorial event was held on the 21st at State Farm Stadium.
- **Record:** Deseret News (S-64) and Fox News (S-57) captions place Kirk's public memorial at State Farm Stadium on Sept. 21, 2025.
  - S-64 para 77 (tier2_reported, Deseret News): "Erika Kirk reacts as she prepares to speak at a memorial for her husband, conservative activist Charlie Kirk, Sunday, Sept. 21, 2025, at State Farm Stadium in Glendale, Ariz."
  - S-57 para 165 (tier2_reported, Fox News): "Andrew Kolvet, producer of "The Charlie Kirk Show," speaks during the public memorial service for Charlie Kirk at State Farm Stadium in Glendale, Arizona, on Sept. 21, 2025."

### C-6264 (ep 70)
- **Claim:** The host says Erika Kirk said in an interview that the next morning Charlie Kirk snuck in and grabbed his wedding band, and the host asks why the ring was off.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-44) quotes Erika Kirk saying that the next morning Charlie came in, grabbed his wedding ring and necklace, and left.
  - S-44 para 163 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): ""He came in and he grabbed that [wedding ring and necklace] and then he left. I didn't even get to give him a kiss goodbye,""

### C-6265 (ep 70)
- **Claim:** The host says Erika Kirk said in her Jesse Watters interview that she wanted Charlie to have a good night's sleep and sent him to sleep in his daughter's room.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-44) reports Erika Kirk told Jesse Watters that Charlie slept in their daughter's bedroom so he could get a good night's sleep.
  - S-44 para 158 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "The night before Charlie was assassinated , Erika and her daughter slept in the couple’s bed. Charlie slept in their daughter’s bedroom."
  - S-44 para 156 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "Charlie Kirk’s widow, Erika Kirk, revealed she has never watched video of her husband’s assassination"

### C-6283 (ep 70)
- **Claim:** The host says Erika Kirk had said she was accompanying her mother as she received a treatment on the morning of September 10.
- **Record:** Erika Kirk told the NYT (S-26) she stayed behind for her mother's medical treatment that day and told Fox News (S-44) she ran out of the treatment center.
  - S-26 para 53 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "It had been Kirk’s plan to accompany her husband to Utah. But her mother would be undergoing medical treatment in the Phoenix area that same day."
  - S-44 para 169 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "I sprinted out of her treatment center and just collapsed in the middle of the parking lot.""

### C-6530 (ep 73)
- **Claim:** In a Fox News interview the host plays, Erika Kirk says Charlie Kirk came into their bedroom bathroom the next morning, where his wedding ring and necklace were, grabbed them and left; the host adds that Erika said he had taken the ring off the night before.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-44) quotes Erika Kirk saying that on the morning he left Charlie came in, grabbed his wedding ring and necklace, and left.
  - S-44 para 163 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): ""He came in and he grabbed that [wedding ring and necklace] and then he left. I didn't even get to give him a kiss goodbye,""

### C-6532 (ep 73)
- **Claim:** In the Fox clip, Erika Kirk says she told Charlie to go sleep in their daughter's room, turning the air down there, so he could get a good night's sleep and be amazing the next day, while she and the daughter stayed in the parents' room.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-44) reports, per Erika Kirk, that Charlie slept in their daughter's bedroom to get a good night's sleep while she and the daughter slept in the couple's bed.
  - S-44 para 158 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "The night before Charlie was assassinated , Erika and her daughter slept in the couple’s bed. Charlie slept in their daughter’s bedroom."

### C-6533 (ep 73)
- **Claim:** In the Fox clip, Erika Kirk says that early on the morning of September 10 Charlie came into their bedroom and bathroom, where his wedding ring and necklace were, picked them up and left, and that she did not get to kiss him goodbye.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-44) quotes Erika Kirk: 'He came in and he grabbed that [wedding ring and necklace] and then he left. I didn't even get to give him a kiss goodbye.'
  - S-44 para 163 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): ""He came in and he grabbed that [wedding ring and necklace] and then he left. I didn't even get to give him a kiss goodbye,""

### C-6539 (ep 73)
- **Claim:** In the Fox clip, Erika Kirk says she had asked Charlie whether he had ever thought about wearing a vest, and that he would nod and say he had looked into it, but always said that if they were going to get him they would.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-44) quotes Erika Kirk asking Charlie about wearing a vest and him saying 'if they're going to get me, they're gonna get me.'
  - S-44 para 177 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): ""I used to say, Charlie, have you ever thought about wearing a vest?""

### C-6540 (ep 73)
- **Claim:** The Fox segment says Erika's mother was dealing with a medical issue so Erika stayed in Phoenix to care for her, and that she planned to join Charlie at the next scheduled event at Colorado State a few days later.
- **Record:** Erika Kirk told the NYT (S-26) she stayed in the Phoenix area for her mother's medical treatment and planned to join Charlie at Colorado State University.
  - S-26 para 53 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "It had been Kirk’s plan to accompany her husband to Utah. But her mother would be undergoing medical treatment in the Phoenix area that same day."
  - S-26 para 53 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "They agreed that she would instead travel with him on the next leg of the tour, to Colorado State University."

### C-7027 (ep 78)
- **Claim:** The host says that, as she said before, the bullet did not go through and did not cause any sort of mess.
- **Record:** Judge Graf's bindover ruling (S-7, 08:16:02) finds the bullet traveled front to back and downward after striking Kirk and the projectile did not exit his body (the 'no mess' aside is not addressed).
  - S-7 (08:16:02) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "the bullet traveled anatomically from front to back and downward after striking Mr. Kirk. The projectile did not exit his body."

### C-7153 (ep 80)
- **Claim:** Joe Kent's letter states he has resigned from the Director of the National Counterterrorism Center effective the day of the letter, citing inability in good conscience to support the ongoing war in Iran.
- **Record:** Deseret News (S-47) reports Joe Kent left as NCTC director over his view that Iran was not an imminent threat; the letter's wording and effective date are not reported.
  - S-47 para 43 (tier2_reported, Deseret News): "Joe Kent, who recently left his position as the director of the federal National Counterterrorism Center, says there’s still a case against alleged Charlie Kirk assassin Tyler Robinson"
  - S-47 para 47 (tier2_reported, Deseret News): "Kent left his position in the Trump administration over claims that Iran was not an imminent threat to the U.S."

### C-7154 (ep 80)
- **Claim:** Kent's resignation letter asserts that "Iran posed no imminent threat to our nation."
- **Record:** Deseret News (S-47) reports Kent left the administration over claims that Iran was not an imminent threat to the U.S.
  - S-47 para 47 (tier2_reported, Deseret News): "Kent left his position in the Trump administration over claims that Iran was not an imminent threat to the U.S."

### C-7247 (ep 81)
- **Claim:** Joe Kent told Tucker Carlson that the National Counterterrorism Center was stopped by the FBI from investigating the Charlie Kirk assassination.
- **Record:** Deseret News (S-47) reports Kent claimed on Carlson's show that the DOJ and FBI barred his NCTC team from investigating Kirk's death.
  - S-47 para 48 (tier2_attributed, Joe Kent): "Kent also claimed on Carlson’s show that when his team tried to investigate the death of Kirk, the Department of Justice and the Federal Bureau of Investigation barred them from doing so."

### C-7350 (ep 82)
- **Claim:** The host says Erika Kirk has said she collapsed in a parking lot while with her mother getting treatment, that there were curiously no witnesses, that the host does not know whether an ambulance was called, and that Erika does not like to speak about it and gives no details.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-44) quotes Erika Kirk saying she sprinted out of her mother's treatment center and collapsed in the parking lot; the host's remarks on witnesses, an ambulance and her reticence are commentary not checked.
  - S-44 para 169 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "I sprinted out of her treatment center and just collapsed in the middle of the parking lot.""

### C-7389 (ep 82)
- **Claim:** Per the host, the N40JD plane, owned by Jason Knupp (who the host says appears to be the chief commander of Naval Surface Force Atlantic), departed Scottsdale about one hour after the shooting and landed at Provo at 2:29 PM MST.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-13) shows N40JD (Premier I, hex a4ab14) taking off from Scottsdale at 19:27:17Z, 64 minutes after the 18:23:28Z shot, and landing at Provo at 20:29:15Z (14:29 MDT); the ownership details are not in this record.
  - S-13 hex a4ab14, 19:27:17Z-20:29:15Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a4ab14 N40JD PRM1: takeoff 19:27:17Z KSDL; landing 20:29:15Z at 40.2269,-111.7295 (KPVU)"

### C-7465 (ep 83)
- **Claim:** The host says authorities are telling the public there is a bullet or fragment they cannot match to the weapon and that they do not know why it did not go through.
- **Record:** The ATF report read into the record (S-5, 05:31:18) says the bullet jacket fragment recovered from Kirk at autopsy cannot be identified or excluded as fired from the rifle.
  - S-5 (05:31:18) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): ""The Exhibit 6 bullet jacket fragment cannot be identified or excluded as having been fired from the Exhibit 1 rifle"
  - S-5 (05:30:42) (testimony, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "Exhibit 6 is the bullet jacket fragment, as well as some other fragments that were recovered from Mr. Kirk at the time of autopsy."

### C-7467 (ep 83)
- **Claim:** In the played clip, a speaker in the Rogan conversation says the round Charlie was supposedly shot with was a .30-06, a round you can kill a moose with, and that it does not have an exit wound.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7, 07:46:29, 08:08:07) finds Kirk was killed by a .30-caliber class round that did not exit his body, with a Mauser .30-06 the suspected murder weapon.
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "Mr. Kirk died by a gunshot wound to his neck. Forensic examination of bullet fragments found in Mr. Kirk's body revealed the bullet to be a 30 caliber class round."
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The bullet did not exit Mr. Kirk's body, but came to rest and tissues in his upper back left of the middle line."
  - S-7 (08:08:07) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "the DNA profiles obtained from the Mauser .30-06 caliber bolt action rifle, the suspected murder weapon, provide forensic evidence connecting defendant to the firearm."

### C-7468 (ep 83)
- **Claim:** In the played clip, a speaker says Joe Kent said they were told to stop their investigation and that it would be handled, and that he then resigned.
- **Record:** Deseret News (S-47) reports Joe Kent said on Carlson's show that DOJ and FBI barred his team from investigating Kirk's death, and that he has left his NCTC post (the reporting attributes his departure to Iran, not the halted inquiry).
  - S-47 para 48 (tier2_attributed, Joe Kent): "Kent also claimed on Carlson’s show that when his team tried to investigate the death of Kirk, the Department of Justice and the Federal Bureau of Investigation barred them from doing so."
  - S-47 para 43 (tier2_reported, Deseret News): "Joe Kent, who recently left his position as the director of the federal National Counterterrorism Center, says there’s still a case against alleged Charlie Kirk assassin Tyler Robinson"

### C-7532 (ep 84)
- **Claim:** The host says the New York Times interview reported that Erika Kirk was in the hospital with her mother on September 10, which the host disputes.
- **Record:** The New York Times interview piece (S-26) reports Erika Kirk was sitting in her mother's hospital room when McCoy's number appeared on Sept. 10, which is what the host says it reported.
  - S-26 para 54 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "Kirk was sitting in her mother’s hospital room when she saw the number of her husband’s longtime assistant, Michael McCoy, appear on her phone."

### C-7535 (ep 84)
- **Claim:** The host reads the New York Times article as stating that Charlie Kirk's chartered plane went back to Scottsdale to ferry his wife to Provo and that he was pronounced dead "as she was airborne," and says the article has never been corrected.
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-26) says Kirk's chartered plane went back to Scottsdale to ferry his wife to Provo and that he was pronounced dead as she was airborne; whether it was ever corrected is not reported.
  - S-26 para 55 (tier2_reported, New York Times): "Charlie Kirk’s chartered plane traveled back to Scottsdale to ferry his wife to Provo. He was pronounced dead as she was airborne."

### C-7556 (ep 84)
- **Claim:** The host says Tyler Robinson has not entered a plea and has not yet had his arraignment.
- **Record:** AP (S-23) and Fox News (S-35) both report that Robinson has not entered a plea.
  - S-23 para 60 (tier2_reported, Associated Press (via KSAT)): "Robinson has not yet entered a plea and his attorneys have not commented on his guilt or innocence."
  - S-35 para 162 (tier2_reported, Fox News): "Robinson is charged with aggravated murder in Kirk’s death, and prosecutors are seeking the death penalty. He has not entered a plea."

### C-7621 (ep 85)
- **Claim:** The host says Sheriff Nate Brooksby initially came out, and that it was to the Washington County Sheriff's Department that Tyler Robinson allegedly turned himself in.
- **Record:** Agents Hull (S-3, 02:23:02) and Davis (S-4, 39:22) testified Robinson went voluntarily to, and was detained at, the Washington County Sheriff's Office; Sheriff Brooksby's role is not covered.
  - S-3 (02:23:02) (testimony, Agent Hull, former Utah State Bureau of Investigation agent (witness; Rev also renders Holt/Hall)): "Yes."
  - S-4 (39:22) (testimony, SBI Agent Brian Davis (witness)): "Yes, he was."

### C-7637 (ep 85)
- **Claim:** The host says Sheriff Brooksby's clip says what she has said: Mike Mitchell came in, there was pressure, and Tyler Robinson agreed to go to the police station because he did not want to be shot by SWAT teams.
- **Record:** Sheriff Brooksby said (S-61) Robinson feared being shot by law enforcement and a SWAT hit and agreed to a peaceful surrender with his family's help; 'pressure' and the Mitchell name are the host's additions.
  - S-61 para 103 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "Robinson was "truly fearful of being shot by law enforcement," as well as fearful of a SWAT team hit on his house, Washington County Sheriff Nate Brooksby said Wednesday during a brief news conference."
  - S-61 para 102 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "was afraid of being shot by police and ultimately agreed to a peaceful surrender with the help of his family, a sheriff in Utah said."

### C-7638 (ep 85)
- **Claim:** Sheriff Brooksby stated that at 8:02 PM on September 11th he received a call from a friend who was a retired detective; court documents refer to this individual as a "retired Washington County deputy."
- **Record:** Brooksby said (S-61) a friend who was a retired detective and former deputy called him on Thursday (Sept. 11); the 8:02 PM time and court-document wording are not in the reporting.
  - S-61 para 105 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): ""I received a call from a friend of mine who happens to be a retired detective that worked for me for many years," Brooksby said."
  - S-61 para 103 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "Brooksby also recounted how a friend and former deputy called him on Thursday"

### C-7641 (ep 85)
- **Claim:** In the Brooksby clip, Brooksby says his friend told him he knew who Charlie Kirk's shooter was, knew the family through religious association, and that they were working on getting him to come in voluntarily.
- **Record:** Brooksby said (S-61) his friend called and said he knew the family through religious association and they were working to get him to come in voluntarily.
  - S-61 para 105 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "I know the family through religious association and he's in Washington County now, and we're working on trying to get him to come in voluntarily.'""
  - S-61 para 103 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "Brooksby also recounted how a friend and former deputy called him on Thursday"

### C-7661 (ep 85)
- **Claim:** Sheriff Brooksby stated that within the hour, his friend drove Tyler Robinson and his parents to Brooksby's office, where Robinson was greeted by plainclothes detectives.
- **Record:** Agent Davis (S-4, 37:00) testified Robinson came to the Washington County Sheriff's Office with his parents and a family friend, and the ruling (S-7, 07:55:06) finds the friend picked him up about 8:30 p.m. to take him there; the plainclothes detail is not covered.
  - S-4 (37:00) (testimony, SBI Agent Brian Davis (witness)): "he'd come there with his parents and a family friend."
  - S-7 (07:55:06) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "At approximately 8:30 PM, a family friend went to pick up Mr. Robinson and take him to the sheriff's office. He asked Mr. Robinson if he had any property or clothes that he used during the incident. Mr. Robinson responded that he had discarded those items."

### C-7662 (ep 85)
- **Claim:** In the Brooksby clip, Brooksby says Tyler knew it was inevitable he would be caught given the law enforcement pressure, with his picture and the gun on the news.
- **Record:** Brooksby said (S-61) Robinson knew it was 'just inevitable' he would be caught after his pictures and the gun were reported in the news.
  - S-61 para 107 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "The sheriff said Robinson knew it was "just inevitable" that he was going to get caught after his pictures and the gun he allegedly used were reported in the news."

### C-7663 (ep 85)
- **Claim:** Sheriff Brooksby stated Tyler Robinson was fearful of a SWAT team hit on his house and fearful of being shot by law enforcement, which was part of the negotiation to bring him in.
- **Record:** Brooksby said (S-61) Robinson was 'truly fearful of being shot by law enforcement' and did not want a SWAT team hitting his parents' house or his apartment.
  - S-61 para 103 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "Robinson was "truly fearful of being shot by law enforcement," as well as fearful of a SWAT team hit on his house, Washington County Sheriff Nate Brooksby said Wednesday during a brief news conference."
  - S-61 para 109 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): ""He didn't want a big SWAT team hitting his parents' house or his apartment."

### C-7669 (ep 85)
- **Claim:** A New York Times article reports that Mr. Kirk's chartered plane traveled back to Scottsdale to ferry his wife to Provo, and that he was pronounced dead as she was airborne.
- **Record:** The New York Times piece (S-26, republished by the Boston Globe) says Kirk's chartered plane went back to Scottsdale to ferry his wife to Provo and he was pronounced dead as she was airborne.
  - S-26 para 55 (tier2_reported, New York Times): "Charlie Kirk’s chartered plane traveled back to Scottsdale to ferry his wife to Provo. He was pronounced dead as she was airborne."
  - S-26 para 64 (tier2_reported, Boston Globe): "This article originally appeared in The New York Times ."

### C-7685 (ep 86)
- **Claim:** The host says Charlie Kirk was shot on September 10 at 12:23 p.m. Mountain Time.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report timeline (S-12 p. 21) records a single gunshot at 12:23 p.m. on September 10 striking Charlie Kirk, and Agent Hull testified (S-3, 00:55:27) the shot was fired at 12:23:28.
  - S-12 p. 21 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "12:23 p.m. A single gunshot was fired, striking Charlie Kirk. UVUPD radio traffic broadcast “shots fired.”"
  - S-3 (00:55:27) (testimony, SBI Agent Hull (witness)): "Just until the reported shot was fired at 12:23:28. The individual then stands up and then moves across the building to the north."

### C-7687 (ep 86)
- **Claim:** The host says Farnsworth runs the audio-visual company Turning Point employs, which she says it may have used for over a decade for its big events, and that he never goes to these college events but, he says, they were trying something different that day.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified (S-2, 05:26:57, 05:28:36) that Farnsworth is from Visual Impulse, TPUSA's event videographer, and believed he is its owner-operator; the decade-long relationship and attendance claims are not covered.
  - S-2 (05:28:36) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (lead case agent, witness)): "It's the 1102 statement that was provided to me, completed by Mr. Farnsworth from Visual Impulse, who is the videographer for TPUSA for the events that they do."
  - S-2 (05:26:57) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (lead case agent, witness)): "I believe he's the owner operator, or the director of the company."

### C-7717 (ep 86)
- **Claim:** Per the defense filing the host shows and reads, the defense seeks to continue the preliminary hearing currently scheduled for May 2026.
- **Record:** KSL (S-38) and KUTV (S-55) report the defense moved to continue the preliminary hearing set for May 2026.
  - S-38 para 16 (tier2_reported, KSL): "Tyler Robinson and his defense team have filed a motion requesting that his preliminary hearing scheduled in May be continued."
  - S-38 para 18 (tier2_reported, KSL): "A preliminary hearing is currently scheduled for May 18, 19 and 21."
  - S-55 para 37 (tier2_reported, KUTV): "A preliminary hearing for Robinson was scheduled in May 2026. However, his attorneys have requested a continuance for time to go over discovery in the case."

### C-7722 (ep 86)
