- **Claim:** The host reads that Robinson has not received the forensic case files and the data underlying the lab reports, and that the defense has been requesting them since September 30.
- **Record:** KSL (S-38) quotes the defense motion saying Robinson has not received the forensic case files and data necessary to investigate; the Sept. 30 request date is not reported.
  - S-38 para 23 (tier2_attributed, Robinson defense motion): "Mr. Robinson has not received the forensic case files and data necessary to investigate"

### C-7730 (ep 86)
- **Claim:** The host reads the filing as saying the FBI and ATF DNA reports recovered minute quantities of DNA on various items that the reports opine are mixtures of up to five or more individuals, and that the state will offer these without supporting expert testimony.
- **Record:** ATF DNA chief Caitlin Oliver agreed (S-6, 01:33:26-01:33:32) that many samples were mixtures of four or five people and (02:02:26) that all samples in one report were mixtures.
  - S-6 (01:33:26) (testimony, defense attorney Michael Burt): "And in many cases, they were mixtures of sometimes four and five people, correct?"
  - S-6 (02:02:26) (testimony, Caitlin Oliver, ATF forensic biologist (witness)): "Correct."

### C-7731 (ep 86)
- **Claim:** Per the defense filing the host reads, the ATF was unable to identify the bullet recovered at autopsy to the rifle allegedly tied to Tyler Robinson, according to an ATF summary report provided to the defense.
- **Record:** The ATF report read into the record (S-5, 05:31:18) says the bullet jacket fragment recovered at autopsy cannot be identified or excluded as fired from the Exhibit 1 rifle.
  - S-5 (05:31:18) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): ""The Exhibit 6 bullet jacket fragment cannot be identified or excluded as having been fired from the Exhibit 1 rifle"
  - S-5 (05:30:42) (testimony, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "Exhibit 6 is the bullet jacket fragment, as well as some other fragments that were recovered from Mr. Kirk at the time of autopsy."

### C-7732 (ep 86)
- **Claim:** The host reads the filing as saying the ATF summary report found the autopsy bullet could not be identified to the rifle allegedly tied to Robinson, that the state has not indicated it will produce the report at the hearing, and that the defense may call the ATF analyst as exculpatory evidence.
- **Record:** The ATF report read into evidence (S-5, 05:31:18) states the bullet jacket fragment recovered from Kirk at autopsy (S-5, 05:30:42) cannot be identified or excluded as fired from the Exhibit 1 rifle, matching the filing's description of the ATF finding.
  - S-5 (05:31:18) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): ""The Exhibit 6 bullet jacket fragment cannot be identified or excluded as having been fired from the Exhibit 1 rifle"
  - S-5 (05:30:42) (testimony, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "Exhibit 6 is the bullet jacket fragment, as well as some other fragments that were recovered from Mr. Kirk at the time of autopsy."

### C-7734 (ep 86)
- **Claim:** Per the defense filing the host reads, the state has indicated that the FBI is in the process of conducting a second comparative bullet analysis and a bullet lead analysis, neither of which is yet complete.
- **Record:** KSL (S-38) quotes the defense motion saying a comparative bullet analysis and bullet lead analysis is still in progress at the FBI.
  - S-38 para 24 (tier2_attributed, Robinson defense motion): "a comparative bullet analysis and bullet lead analysis that is still in progress at the FBI"

### C-7740 (ep 86)
- **Claim:** The host reads the filing as asserting that in a capital case the state cannot, consistent with due process and the Fourth and Eighth Amendments and separation of powers, rely on hearsay summary reports, and she says the defense has repeatedly asked for the underlying science.
- **Record:** The contempt-hearing ruling (S-8, 18:54) records the defense argument that proving probable cause by hearsay violates the 4th, 6th, 8th and 14th Amendments and separation of powers.
  - S-8 (18:54) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "Defendant argues that permitting the State to establish probable cause through hearsay evidence violates his rights under the 4th, 6th, 8th, and 14th amendments to the United States Constitution as well as separation of powers principles."

### C-7834 (ep 87)
- **Claim:** The host says the ATF says its bullet comparison is inconclusive, which she calls the best they could do.
- **Record:** The ATF toolmark report (S-5, 05:31:18) and examiner Karner (S-5, 07:18:43) state the bullet-fragment-to-rifle comparison was inconclusive.
  - S-5 (05:31:18) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "based on an agreement of all discernible class characteristics and neither sufficient agreement nor sufficient disagreement of individual characteristics. The results of the comparison was inconclusive.""
  - S-5 (07:18:43) (testimony, Samantha Karner, ATF firearm and toolmark examiner (witness)): "30X refers to the magnification that the photo was taken at. And then INC refers to inconclusive, the final conclusion."

### C-7905 (ep 88)
- **Claim:** The host reads the New York Times saying Erika planned to go to Utah, but her mother would undergo medical treatment in the Phoenix area that day and Charlie told her home needed her, so she would join the Colorado State leg instead.
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-26) reports Erika planned to go to Utah but her mother would undergo medical treatment in the Phoenix area that day, so she would join the Colorado State leg instead.
  - S-26 para 53 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "It had been Kirk’s plan to accompany her husband to Utah. But her mother would be undergoing medical treatment in the Phoenix area that same day."
  - S-26 para 53 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "They agreed that she would instead travel with him on the next leg of the tour, to Colorado State University."

### C-7907 (ep 88)
- **Claim:** The host reads the New York Times saying Erika saw Michael McCoy's number appear and that, in retrospect, she said she knew the words before he screamed them.
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-26) reports Erika saw McCoy's number appear and said she knew the words 'He's been shot!' before he screamed them.
  - S-26 para 54 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "Kirk was sitting in her mother’s hospital room when she saw the number of her husband’s longtime assistant, Michael McCoy, appear on her phone."
  - S-26 para 54 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "she said she knew the words — “He’s been shot!” — before McCoy screamed them."

### C-7910 (ep 88)
- **Claim:** In the Jesse Watters clip, Erika Kirk says she collapsed in the middle of the parking lot after the call and the host's comments follow on that.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-44) quotes Erika Kirk saying she 'just collapsed in the middle of the parking lot.'
  - S-44 para 169 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "I sprinted out of her treatment center and just collapsed in the middle of the parking lot.""

### C-8068 (ep 90)
- **Claim:** The host says Sheriff Brooksby then called the Utah County Sheriff Mike Smith to tell him about the call he received from Mike Mitchell.
- **Record:** Brooksby said (S-61) that after a friend and former deputy called him he called the Utah County Sheriff to alert lead investigators; the names Mitchell and Smith are not in this reporting.
  - S-61 para 106 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "Brooksby said he then called the Utah County Sheriff to alert the lead investigators."
  - S-61 para 103 (tier2_attributed, Nate Brooksby): "Brooksby also recounted how a friend and former deputy called him on Thursday"

### C-8069 (ep 90)
- **Claim:** The host says that according to Sheriff Nate Brooksby's press conference Tyler arrived at the police station at 9:00, which she says does not track with the other times, and that Kash Patel later said Robinson was booked in the 33rd hour.
- **Record:** Agent Davis testified (S-5, 02:53:36) and Judge Graf found (S-7, 07:56:00) that Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office at approximately 9:00 PM (the Patel '33rd hour' remark is not addressed).
  - S-5 (02:53:36) (testimony, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "Yes, that's correct."
  - S-7 (07:56:00) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "At approximately 9:00 PM, Mr. Robinson arrived at the Washington County Sheriff's Office. He was wearing a maroon shirt and jeans."

### C-8074 (ep 90)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Governor Spencer Cox says investigators identified Robinson's roommate, who said Robinson made a joke on Discord, then opened Discord and allowed investigators to photograph the screen as each message was shown.
- **Record:** Agent Davis testified (S-5, 02:51:08-02:51:15) that Exhibit 16.2 is the Discord messages Twiggs showed, photographed on his phone by an FBI agent at his first interview.
  - S-5 (02:51:08) (testimony, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "These are the Discord messages that Lance had talked about."
  - S-5 (02:21:58) (exhibit, Lance Twiggs (sworn recorded statement, State's Exhibit 16)): "Yeah, this is Discord."

### C-8075 (ep 90)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Governor Spencer Cox says the messages from the contact Tyler stated a need to retrieve a rifle from a drop point, leaving the rifle in a bush, watching the area where it was left, and leaving it wrapped in a towel.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7, 07:52:46, 08:24:13) cites texts saying he left the rifle wrapped in a towel in a bush where he changed outfits, and the Information (S-1, p. 8) quotes his plan to grab it from his drop point.
  - S-7 (07:52:46) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The texter explained that he was still in Orem because he had left his rifle wrapped in a towel in a bush where he changed outfits."
  - S-7 (08:24:13) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "text messages attributed to him in which he stated he had concealed his rifle by leaving it in a bush where he changed outfits."
  - S-1 p. 8 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "I had planned to grab my rifle from my drop point shortly after, but most of that side of town got locked down."

### C-8076 (ep 90)
- **Claim:** In a Discord statement published in The Hill on September 12, 2025, which the host reads, Discord says it identified an account associated with the suspect but found no evidence the suspect planned the incident or promoted violence on Discord.
- **Record:** Reporting (S-16) quotes Discord's statement that it found 'no evidence that the suspect planned this incident or promoted violence on Discord.'
  - S-16 para 37 (tier2_attributed, Discord): ""no evidence that the suspect planned this incident or promoted violence on Discord.""

### C-8077 (ep 90)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Governor Spencer Cox says the messages also referred to engraving bullets, a scope and the rifle being unique, and that the contact Tyler said he had changed outfits.
- **Record:** Exhibit 16 texts (S-5, 02:18:28, 02:46:40) mention engraving bullets and leaving the rifle where he changed outfits, and the ruling (S-7, 07:52:46) quotes a text about the rifle's 2K scope.
  - S-5 (02:18:28) (exhibit, prosecutor Ryan McBride (interviewer, State's Exhibit 16)): "He asks you here, "Remember how I was engraving bullets?""
  - S-7 (07:52:46) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): ""Judging from today, I would say grandpa's gun does just fine. IDK. "I think it was a $2,000 scope or a 2K scope."
  - S-5 (02:46:40) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "I had to leave it in a bush where I changed outfits. Didn't have the ability or time to bring it with.""

- **Claim:** The host says Erika Kirk was scheduled to appear alongside Vice President JD Vance at the University of Georgia event the night before.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-57) reports Erika Kirk was scheduled to appear with JD Vance at the Turning Point USA event in Athens, and she wrote on X she was looking forward to the University of Georgia event with Vance.
  - S-57 para 161 (tier2_reported, Fox News): "Kirk was scheduled to appear alongside Vice President JD Vance at the Turning Point USA event in Athens, Georgia, on Tuesday, but ultimately withdrew following the threats ."
  - S-57 para 171 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "I was so looking forward to tonight’s event at the @universityofga with our Vice President @JDVance"

### C-8208 (ep 92)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Andrew Kolvet announces on stage at the UGA event that Erika Kirk is absent because she has received "very serious threats" in her direction.
- **Record:** Reporting (S-56) says Kolvet said at the event that Erika Kirk had received 'some very serious threats in her direction' that led her to cancel.
  - S-56 para 107 (tier2_attributed, Andrew Kolvet): "Turning Point executive Andrew Kolvet said that Kirk had received "some very serious threats in her direction" that led her to cancel her plans to appear with the vice president."

### C-8213 (ep 92)
- **Claim:** Andrew Kolvet stated on Fox News that TPUSA's security team told the organization "we cannot guarantee your safety" regarding Erika Kirk.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-57) quotes Kolvet saying the security team told them, 'We cannot guarantee your safety, we recommend that you don't do this.'
  - S-57 para 157 (tier2_attributed, Andrew Kolvet): ""To see the security team tell us, 'We cannot guarantee your safety, we recommend that you don't do this,' was a really chilling moment for all of us"

### C-8214 (ep 92)
- **Claim:** On Fox News, Andrew Kolvet said that cancelling Erika Kirk's appearance was, to his knowledge, the first time TPUSA has had to make such a decision, and pointed to what happened to TPUSA front-lines reporter Savanah Hernandez in Minneapolis.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-57) quotes Kolvet calling it the first time Turning Point had to make that decision and citing the attack on TPUSA Frontlines reporter Savanah Hernandez in Minneapolis.
  - S-57 para 166 (tier2_attributed, Andrew Kolvet): ""I think it's the first time that we've ever had to make that decision in Turning Point history," he said."
  - S-57 para 167 (tier2_attributed, Andrew Kolvet): "you saw what happened with Savanah Hernandez, one of our TPUSA Frontlines reporters in Minneapolis," he added, referring to an attack on Hernandez earlier this week."

### C-8225 (ep 92)
- **Claim:** Erika Kirk posted on X that she cancelled because she "takes [her] security team's recommendations extremely seriously."
- **Record:** Fox News (S-57) quotes Erika Kirk's X post: 'I take my security team's recommendations extremely seriously.'
  - S-57 para 171 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "after all our family has been through, I take my security team’s recommendations extremely seriously."

### C-8271 (ep 92)
- **Claim:** The host says George Zinn, whom she calls the decoy boy, was arrested for having pornographic images of children on his phone.
- **Record:** KSL (S-30) reports Zinn, arrested after the shooting, was charged with sexual exploitation of a minor after investigators found inappropriate images of children on his phone (his arrest at the scene was for obstruction).
  - S-30 para 12 (tier2_reported, KSL (photo caption)): "George Zinn, 71, who was arrested after Charlie Kirk was shot at Utah Valley University in Orem on Sept. 10, was charged this week with obstruction of justice and sexual exploitation of a minor."
  - S-30 para 30 (tier2_attributed, charging documents): "Investigators went through the phone and found multiple inappropriate images containing children in his sent folder, charges state."

### C-8301 (ep 93)
- **Claim:** JD Vance told Andrew on stage that "about 2 hours ago… I was a little worried that we were going to have to cancel the event because Erika was not going to come."
- **Record:** Reporting (S-56) says Vance told the crowd he had been worried the event would be canceled altogether.
  - S-56 para 108 (tier2_attributed, JD Vance): "Vance said he'd been worried that the event would be canceled altogether."

### C-8303 (ep 93)
- **Claim:** J.D. Vance says on stage that he talked to the Secret Service, who do a very good job, and told them to let Erika do what she needs to do for herself and her family.
- **Record:** Reporting (S-56) quotes Vance telling the crowd he talked to the Secret Service, who 'do a very good job,' and said to let Erika do what she needs to do for herself and her family.
  - S-56 para 109 (tier2_attributed, JD Vance): ""I talked to the Secret Service, and obviously, these guys do a very good job. And I said, 'You know what? Let's let Erika do what she needs to do for herself and her family,'" Vance told the crowd."

### C-8348 (ep 93)
- **Claim:** Per the ATF summary the host shows and discusses, the cartridge cases matched the exhibit 1 rifle.
- **Record:** The ATF report read into the record (S-5, 05:29:01, 05:39:06) identifies the Exhibit 2, 16 and 18A-18E cartridge cases as fired in the Exhibit 1 rifle.
  - S-5 (05:39:06) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): ""The Exhibit 16 and Exhibit 18A through 18E cartridge cases were identified as having been fired in the Exhibit 1 rifle.""
  - S-5 (05:29:01) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): ""The Exhibit 2 cartridge case was identified as having been fired in the Exhibit 1 rifle.""

### C-8349 (ep 93)
- **Claim:** ATF summary stated fragments from bullet jackets could not be identified or excluded as fired from exhibit 1 based on an agreement of all discernable class characteristics.
- **Record:** The ATF report (S-5, 05:31:18) says the bullet jacket fragment could not be identified or excluded as fired from the Exhibit 1 rifle, based on agreement of all discernible class characteristics.
  - S-5 (05:31:18) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): ""The Exhibit 6 bullet jacket fragment cannot be identified or excluded as having been fired from the Exhibit 1 rifle"
  - S-5 (05:31:18) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "based on an agreement of all discernible class characteristics and neither sufficient agreement nor sufficient disagreement of individual characteristics. The results of the comparison was inconclusive.""

### C-8351 (ep 93)
- **Claim:** Per the ATF summary the host shows and discusses, the ATF is to conduct a microscopic comparison on the bullet evidence.
- **Record:** The ATF report read into evidence (S-5, 05:31:18-05:31:54) records a microscopic comparison of the bullet jacket fragment with an inconclusive result (lead fragments unsuitable), and ATF examiner Karner testified (S-5, 06:52:49) to microscopic marks of value for comparison.
  - S-5 (05:31:18) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "based on an agreement of all discernible class characteristics and neither sufficient agreement nor sufficient disagreement of individual characteristics. The results of the comparison was inconclusive.""
  - S-5 (05:31:54) (exhibit, Sgt. Faumuina, Utah State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): ""The Exhibit 6B through 6E lead fragments are not suitable for microscopic comparison.""
  - S-5 (06:52:49) (testimony, Samantha Karner, ATF firearm and toolmark examiner (defense witness)): "That there were microscopic marks of value worthy of comparison or yes, individual characteristics in the land and groove impressions."

### C-8356 (ep 93)
- **Claim:** The host says TPUSA ignored the security concerns expressed by the school that the event should not be held in an uncontrolled traffic area, and put Charlie there like a sitting duck.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12 pp. 48, 64) finds UVU expressed concerns about the controllability of the outdoor location but agreed to TPUSA's requested venue when TPUSA's Maycee Crofts insisted (the 'sitting duck' remark is characterisation).
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "multiple interviews the Project Team conducted indicated that UVU had expressed concerns about the outdoor location, including during the August 25 MEAC meeting, but ultimately agreed to TPUSA’s requested venue location when TPUSA representative Maycee Crofts insisted on it."
  - S-12 p. 64 (report_finding, Utah Valley University After-Action Report (Project Team)): "UVU participants raised concerns during the MEAC process regarding the controllability of the requested outdoor location and suggested an alternative location where access could be more effectively managed."

### C-8387 (ep 94)
- **Claim:** Defense attorney Richard Novak states the defense has not received the data files (electronic quantification/digitization of physical evidence) needed to analyze the forensic examiners' statistical results, saying the ATF provided material only in the last 3 weeks and the FBI still has not.
- **Record:** ABC4 (S-39) reports Novak said the FBI had not turned over any data, the ATF only recently turned over its files, and the defense lacks the raw data behind the federal summary reports.
  - S-39 para 27 (tier2_attributed, Richard Novak): "He also asked the court why the FBI has not turned over any data yet and why the ATF only recently turned over those files, something he said the state has not made clear."
  - S-39 para 29 (tier2_attributed, Richard Novak): "The prosecutors have the summary reports from federal law enforcement, but not the raw data."
  - S-39 para 43 (tier2_attributed, Richard Novak): "He said that they have yet to receive the physical and electronic evidence that is the basis of the probable cause statement used to charge Robinson."

### C-8389 (ep 94)
- **Claim:** Richard Novak says he is aware of no reason why the ATF provided its data only within the last three weeks and why the FBI still has not provided its data.
- **Record:** ABC4 (S-39) reports Novak asked why the FBI had not turned over any data and why the ATF only recently did, saying the state had not made this clear.
  - S-39 para 27 (tier2_attributed, Richard Novak): "He also asked the court why the FBI has not turned over any data yet and why the ATF only recently turned over those files, something he said the state has not made clear."

### C-8409 (ep 94)
- **Claim:** The host says Tyler Robinson is a 22-year-old facing the death sentence.
- **Record:** KSL (S-41) and the Deseret News (S-31) report Robinson faces a possible death sentence if convicted; his exact age at the time of the episode is not checked.
  - S-41 para 19 (tier2_reported, KSL): "Robinson faces a possible death sentence if convicted."
  - S-31 para 44 (tier2_reported, Deseret News): "Robinson faces a possible death sentence if convicted."

### C-8506 (ep 95)
- **Claim:** The host says that at the UVU event Charlie Kirk sat on a chair on a platform that put him above the table. She says that in years of tabling with him across the United States they were never on platforms.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7, 07:44:38) finds Kirk was seated on a raised chair under the tent behind a U-shaped table; the host's tabling history is not covered.
  - S-7 (07:44:38) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "At approximately 12:20, Mr. Kirk was seated on the raised chair under the middle of the tent behind a U-shaped table"

### C-8521 (ep 95)
- **Claim:** The host reads Blake Neff's X post saying the charging document shows Tyler Robinson's parents did not recognize the gun but recognized FBI-released photos of the suspect. The post says only then did they suspect the rifle was a match, after the FBI had announced finding a bolt-action rifle.
- **Record:** The charging Information (S-1 pp. 6-7) states Robinson's mother saw the shooter's photo in the news and thought it looked like her son, the father agreed, and the father then believed the suspected rifle matched one given to his son as a gift, matching the post's account of the document.
  - S-1 p. 6 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson’s mother saw the photo of the shooter in the news and thought the shooter looked like her son."
  - S-1 p. 6 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson’s mother expressed concern to her husband that the suspected shooter looked like Robinson. Robinson’s father agreed."
  - S-1 p. 7 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "He also believed that the rifle that police suspected the shooter used matched a rifle that was given to his son as a gift."

### C-8523 (ep 95)
- **Claim:** The host reads a Tyler Robinson text message from the charging document saying his dad wanted photos of the rifle and the feds had released a photo of it. She argues this made it fair for media to conclude his father recognized the rifle.
- **Record:** The charging Information (S-1, p. 9) quotes Robinson texting that his dad wanted photos of the rifle and that the feds had released a photo of the very unique rifle.
  - S-1 p. 9 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "Robinson: my dad wants photos of the rifle"
  - S-1 p. 9 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "the feds released a photo of the rifle, and it is very unique. Hes calling me rn, not answering."

### C-8547 (ep 96)
- **Claim:** Trump's Truth Social post called Candace Owens "an extremely low IQ individual" and described her attack on the First Lady of France as "despicable."
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-66) reports Trump posted on Truth Social that Owens is an 'extremely Low IQ individual' and called her claims about Brigitte Macron 'despicable.'
  - S-66 para 25 (tier2_attributed, Donald Trump): "Trump got involved, posting on Truth Social that Owens is an “extremely Low IQ individual” and sharing a doctored image that referred to Owens as “vile person of the year.”"
  - S-66 para 25 (tier2_attributed, Donald Trump): "He also called Owens’ claims about Brigitte Macron “despicable.”"

### C-8636 (ep 97)
- **Claim:** Brian Harpole filed a 69-page civil complaint against Candace Owens asserting multiple defamation claims.
- **Record:** The New York Times (S-66) reports Harpole sued Owens for defamation in a 69-page lawsuit.
  - S-66 para 9 (tier2_reported, The New York Times): "The former head of security for Charlie Kirk sued podcaster Candace Owens on Thursday for defamation, claiming that she falsely implicated him in a conspiracy to assassinate Kirk."
  - S-66 para 12 (tier2_reported, The New York Times): "In the 69-page lawsuit, Harpole, who owns a private security firm and worked for Kirk for several years"

### C-8652 (ep 97)
- **Claim:** The host says a pilot who runs drone training told her that the Provo airspace restriction applies around airports and hangars, but that drones can be flown in Provo with clearance as needed.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, p. 48) finds drone operations are restricted within a five-mile radius of Provo Airport in Class D airspace, and UVU is at least six miles away.
  - S-12 p. 48 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "drone operations are restricted within a five-mile radius of Provo Airport in Class D airspace. UVU is located in the City of Orem, which borders the City of Provo, and is located at least six miles from Provo Airport."

### C-8673 (ep 97)
- **Claim:** The host says Harpole presented the text chain on Shawn Ryan as if it were his own and explicitly said 'I', implying it was why the rooftops were not secured, though it was between Dan Flood and Chief Long.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, pp. 43, 70) shows the roof-access text exchange was between TPUSA's Dan Flood and Chief Long; how Harpole presented it is not covered.
  - S-12 p. 43 (exhibit, Chief Long and Dan Flood (text exchange, Sept 8, 2025)): "Chief Long: You want access to the roof?"
  - S-12 p. 43 (exhibit, Chief Long (text to Dan Flood, Sept 8, 2025)): "Chief Long: I got you covered"
  - S-12 p. 70 (report_finding, Utah Valley University After-Action Report (Project Team)): "On September 8 and 10, Chief Long coordinated directly with TPUSA Security Supervisor Dan Flood by text."

### C-8674 (ep 97)
- **Claim:** The host says that although Harpole presented the Sorensen Center rooftop text chain on Shawn Ryan as his own, the lawsuit admits the messages were between Dan Flood and UVU Police Chief Long.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, pp. 43, 70) shows the roof-access text messages were exchanged between TPUSA's Dan Flood and UVU Police Chief Long.
  - S-12 p. 43 (exhibit, Chief Long and Dan Flood (text exchange, Sept 8, 2025)): "Chief Long: You want access to the roof?"
  - S-12 p. 70 (report_finding, Utah Valley University After-Action Report (Project Team)): "On September 8 and 10, Chief Long coordinated directly with TPUSA Security Supervisor Dan Flood by text."

### C-9114 (ep 102)
- **Claim:** The host says that the day after her first presentation, Erika went on Harris Faulkner's Fox show and for the first time was fired up, telling people to stop conspiracy theories.
- **Record:** Fox News (S-36) quotes Erika Kirk telling Harris Faulkner that her message to conspiracy theorists is "to stop. To stop."; the 'very next day' timing is not verified.
  - S-36 para 15 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "telling Fox News’ Harris Faulkner she doesn’t have the time to "address the noise.""
  - S-36 para 31 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "And so, my message to them is to stop. To stop."

### C-9152 (ep 103)
- **Claim:** The host says President Trump suddenly posted her face on Time magazine calling her the most vile person of the year.
- **Record:** The NYT (S-66) reports Trump posted on Truth Social a doctored image calling Owens "vile person of the year."
  - S-66 para 25 (tier2_attributed, Donald Trump): "Trump got involved, posting on Truth Social that Owens is an “extremely Low IQ individual” and sharing a doctored image that referred to Owens as “vile person of the year.”"

### C-9167 (ep 103)
- **Claim:** The host says that after Charis Bible College, Charlie Kirk was due to meet Erika Kirk at Colorado State University in Fort Collins, which she says Erika Kirk confirmed to Jesse Watters.
- **Record:** Erika Kirk is reported (S-26) as saying she and Charlie agreed she would join him on the next leg of the tour at Colorado State University; that she said so to Jesse Watters is not verified.
  - S-26 para 53 (tier2_attributed, Erika Kirk): "They agreed that she would instead travel with him on the next leg of the tour, to Colorado State University."

### C-9171 (ep 103)
- **Claim:** The host says flight data shows N582MM departed Denver at 11:58 a.m. Mountain Time, 25 minutes before Charlie Kirk was shot, and landed at a military base in Pierre, South Dakota, at 12:56 p.m.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-13) shows N582MM (Learjet 60, hex a77e75) taking off from Rocky Mountain Metropolitan Airport near Denver at 17:58:38Z (11:58 MDT) and landing at Pierre, South Dakota at about 18:55Z (12:55 MDT); whether Pierre is a military base is not in this record.
  - S-13 hex a77e75, 17:58:38Z-18:55:45Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a77e75 N582MM LJ60: takeoff 17:58:38Z at 39.9125,-105.1231 (KBJC); last approach point 18:55:45Z at 44.390,-100.299 (KPIR); next takeoff 19:40:52Z KPIR"

### C-9172 (ep 103)
- **Claim:** The host says N582MM stayed in South Dakota about 50 minutes, flew to Chicago for an apparent pickup, then landed in Provo at 6:57 p.m. and stayed overnight.
- **Record:** ADS-B shows N582MM on the ground at Pierre for about 45 minutes (S-13: 12:55-13:40 MDT), then flying to Chicago Executive, and (S-14) landing at Provo at 00:57Z on 11 September (18:57 MDT on 10 September), where it stayed until the next morning.
  - S-13 hex a77e75, 18:55:45Z-21:04Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a77e75 N582MM: last point 18:55:45Z KPIR; takeoff 19:40:52Z KPIR; 21:04:05Z at 42.135,-87.913 (KPWK); takeoff ~22:00Z westbound"
  - S-14 hex a77e75, 2025-09-11 00:57Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a77e75 N582MM LJ60: landing 2025-09-11 00:57Z at KPVU; next takeoff 14:08Z KPVU"

### C-9173 (ep 103)
- **Claim:** The host says that after staying overnight in Provo on September 10, N582MM flew back to Pierre, South Dakota.
- **Record:** ADS-B (S-14) shows N582MM leaving Provo at 14:08Z on 11 September (08:08 MDT) after the overnight stop and landing at Pierre, South Dakota at about 15:47Z (09:47 MDT).
  - S-14 hex a77e75, 2025-09-11 14:08Z-15:47Z (flight_data, adsb.lol globe history (readsb)): "a77e75 N582MM: takeoff 2025-09-11 14:08Z KPVU; landing ~15:47Z at KPIR"

### C-9501 (ep 108)
- **Claim:** The host says Frank Turek and the security team took Charlie Kirk to the hospital and so should have been first to know he was dead.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, p. 52) and Agent Hull (S-2, 05:58:03) confirm Kirk's security team transported him to Timpanogos Hospital; Turek's role is not covered.
  - S-12 p. 52 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "the Station Three fire truck passed near the SUV being used by the TPUSA security team to transport Charlie Kirk to Timpanogos Hospital."
  - S-2 (05:58:03) (testimony, SBI Agent David Hull (witness)): "Mr. Kirk's detail, his own security team removed him from the scene whilst administering first aid and they conducted what would be called a hasty transport to Timpanogos Hospital."

### C-9502 (ep 108)
- **Claim:** The host says she is currently being sued by Brian Harpole, whom she calls head of Charlie Kirk's security team.
- **Record:** The NYT (S-66) reports Kirk's former head of security sued Owens for defamation, and the Salt Lake Tribune (S-59) and KSL (S-58) identify Brian Harpole as head of Kirk's security team.
  - S-66 para 9 (tier2_reported, The New York Times): "The former head of security for Charlie Kirk sued podcaster Candace Owens on Thursday for defamation, claiming that she falsely implicated him in a conspiracy to assassinate Kirk."
  - S-59 para 10 (tier2_reported, Salt Lake Tribune): "Harpole, who headed the private security team for the conservative commentator."
  - S-58 para 17 (tier2_reported, KSL): "Harpole was head of Kirk’s security for the American Comeback Tour on Utah Valley University campus on Sept. 10."

### C-9532 (ep 108)
- **Claim:** The host shows the filing, which lists as item 31 the FBI DNA report on the towel and the screwdriver among the evidence the defense wants sealed.
- **Record:** The record (S-7, 06:17:24; S-3, 05:06:06) identifies State's Exhibit 31 as the FBI DNA report on the towel and the screwdriver.
  - S-7 (06:17:24) (exhibit, prosecutor Chad Grunander): "I'm looking at State's Exhibit 31. This is the FBI report and the FBI did the analysis on the towel and the screwdriver."
  - S-3 (05:06:06) (testimony, Sgt. Jennifer Faumuina, Utah DPS State Bureau of Investigation (witness)): "Yes, it does."

### C-9663 (ep 110)
- **Claim:** Per local news in Utah cited by host, Turning Point USA submitted on July 18, 2025 for Charlie Kirk to do his event at Utah Valley; host states she has been unable to independently confirm.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report timeline (S-12, p. 18) records that on July 18 the UVU TPUSA Club submitted a formal event request for a Charlie Kirk appearance.
  - S-12 p. 18 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report (Lake Forest Group / Brownstein Hyatt Farber Schreck)): "July 18 Formal event request submitted by UVU TPUSA Club. Event described as an appearance by Charlie Kirk to discuss current events. External ticketing by TPUSA anticipated."

### C-10039 (ep 117)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, a speaker (apparently Frank Turek) says that when he got back into the car he found Charlie Kirk's cross-and-medallion necklace draped over his bag, that a police officer said they had been looking for it, and that he took it.
- **Record:** Turek is reported (S-27) as saying he found Kirk's missing cross necklace draped over his own computer bag; the police-officer detail is not covered.
  - S-27 para 185 (tier2_attributed, Frank Turek): "Turek found the cross draped over his own computer bag, which Kirk was lying on as the team drove him to the hospital."
  - S-27 para 184 (tier2_attributed, Frank Turek): "Turek said Erika asked for her husband's cross necklace, which went missing during the rush."

### C-10050 (ep 117)
- **Claim:** The host says there was unusually no ambulance on standby at the UVU event, and that she always had an ambulance on standby at her own Turning Point USA tour events.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, p. 59) finds no ambulance was staged on site for the September 10 TPUSA event; the host's own past events are not covered.
  - S-12 p. 59 (report_finding, Utah Valley University After-Action Report (Project Team)): "No ambulance was staged on site for the September 10 TPUSA event."

### C-10093 (ep 117)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Frank Turek says that on the drive to the hospital he said they had to start CPR, and that they gave Charlie Kirk CPR but nothing was happening.
- **Record:** Turek is reported (S-27) as saying he and the security team sped to the hospital while performing CPR and praying.
  - S-27 para 167 (tier2_attributed, Frank Turek): "Turek and the security team jumped into the SUV that they used to travel to the event and sped to a hospital while performing CPR and praying."

### C-10105 (ep 117)
- **Claim:** The host says Erika Kirk's pageant background dates from when Trump co-owned the Miss Universe organization with NBC Universal. She suggests Erika knew Trump before Charlie Kirk did.
- **Record:** The NYT (S-26) reports Erika Kirk and Trump have known each other since 2012, when Trump ran the Miss USA pageants and she was Miss Arizona; the NBC co-ownership detail and the host's inference are not covered.
  - S-26 para 47 (tier2_reported, New York Times): "The two have known each other since 2012, when Trump was running the Miss USA beauty pageants and she was Miss Arizona."

### C-10229 (ep 120)
- **Claim:** The host says Brian Harpole joined Shawn Ryan for what she calls a "car crash interview," and that she is being sued over it.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, p. 47) confirms Harpole appeared on the Shawn Ryan Podcast (SRS #254) on November 17, 2025; the lawsuit and characterisation are not covered.
  - S-12 p. 47 (report_finding, UVU After-Action Report Project Team): "appearance on the Shawn Ryan Podcast, titled “Brian Harpole – Groundbreaking Evidence From Charlie Kirk’s Head of Security / SRS #254."

### C-10230 (ep 120)
- **Claim:** Brian Harpole states in the Shawn Ryan interview that he personally cut Charlie Kirk's white "freedom shirt" off at the hospital.
- **Record:** KSL (S-58) reports Harpole said on the Shawn Ryan podcast that after Kirk was taken into a room at Timpanogos Regional Hospital he cut Kirk's Freedom T-shirt off for a defibrillator.
  - S-58 para 21 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "Then Harpole said he cut Kirk’s Freedom T-shirt off to prep him for a defibrillator."
  - S-58 para 21 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "When they got to Timpanogos Regional Hospital in Orem, the team carried Kirk inside, put him on a gurney and took him into a room."
  - S-58 para 17 (tier2_reported, KSL): "In a podcast interview with retired Navy SEAL Shawn Ryan, Brian Harpole described the moments following the shot that killed conservative activist Charlie Kirk."

### C-10231 (ep 120)
- **Claim:** In the Shawn Ryan clip, Harpole says he put Charlie on a gurney, wheeled him into a room, began giving patient information to the staff, and cut the shirt off so they could put a defibrillator on him.
- **Record:** KSL (S-58) reports Harpole said they put Kirk on a gurney, took him into a room, and he cut the Freedom T-shirt off for a defibrillator; the patient-information detail is not covered.
  - S-58 para 21 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "When they got to Timpanogos Regional Hospital in Orem, the team carried Kirk inside, put him on a gurney and took him into a room."
  - S-58 para 21 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "Then Harpole said he cut Kirk’s Freedom T-shirt off to prep him for a defibrillator."

### C-10232 (ep 120)
- **Claim:** In the clip, Harpole says he went back and forth with staff on drugs and the defibrillator, then walked out and held guard on the outside door of the room so nobody else could come in.
- **Record:** KSL (S-58) and the Salt Lake Tribune (S-59) report Harpole said he left the hospital room and stood guard at the door so nobody else could go in.
  - S-58 para 21 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "So he left the room and stood guard, so nobody else could go in."
  - S-59 para 28 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "When they arrived at the Timpanogos Regional Hospital, Harpole remembered placing Kirk on a gurney and trying to help medical staff there. Then, he stood guard at the door."

### C-10256 (ep 120)
- **Claim:** The host says there was unusually no ambulance on standby at the event.
- **Record:** The UVU After-Action Report (S-12, p. 59) finds no ambulance was staged on site for the September 10 TPUSA event.
  - S-12 p. 59 (report_finding, Utah Valley University After-Action Report (Project Team)): "No ambulance was staged on site for the September 10 TPUSA event."

### C-10260 (ep 120)
- **Claim:** The host says Judge Graf has denied the defense motion to compel Lance Twiggs to appear in person at the preliminary hearing set for the week of July 6.
- **Record:** Judge Graf denied the defense application to compel Twiggs's live testimony and quashed the subpoena (S-8, 26:26, 43:33); the preliminary hearing ran July 6-10, 2026 (S-7, 07:41:21).
  - S-8 (43:33) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "the defendant's application for certificate of attendance of out-of-state witness is respectfully denied and the State's motion to quash the subpoena is granted."
  - S-8 (26:26) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "Defendant seeks to compel the live testimony of Lance Twiggs at the upcoming preliminary hearing. The State opposes the application and seeks to quash the subpoena."
  - S-7 (07:41:21) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The court acting as a magistrate conducted a five-day preliminary hearing from July 6th to July 10th of this year."

### C-10262 (ep 120)
- **Claim:** The host says that, under the judge's ruling, Lance Twiggs's testimony against Tyler Robinson will be presented at the preliminary hearing as a recorded statement rather than live testimony.
- **Record:** The S-8 ruling (27:18, 43:33) denied compelling Twiggs and records that the State would rely on his video-recorded Rule 1102 interview as reliable hearsay.
  - S-8 (43:33) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "the defendant's application for certificate of attendance of out-of-state witness is respectfully denied and the State's motion to quash the subpoena is granted."
  - S-8 (27:18) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "the State plans to establish probable cause through reliable hearsay, specifically a video recorded interview of Mr. Twiggs conducted pursuant to Utah Rule of Evidence 1102."

### C-10264 (ep 120)
- **Claim:** The host says Robinson's team argued they should be able to cross-examine Twiggs, otherwise his recorded statement is hearsay.
- **Record:** The Deseret News (S-31) and KSL (S-41) report that Robinson's defense says the recorded Twiggs interview prevents them from cross-examining him; the word 'hearsay' is not quoted.
  - S-31 para 49 (tier2_attributed, Robinson defense): "Robinson’s defense team, however, says that it prevents them from cross-examining Twiggs, whom they describe as “central” to the state’s case."
  - S-41 para 24 (tier2_attributed, defense): "Robinson's defense team, however, says that it prevents them from cross-examining Twiggs, whom they describe as "central" to the state's case."

### C-10313 (ep 121)
- **Claim:** Brian Harpole states in his account that he got on top of Charlie on a gurney in the ER and cut his white "Freedom" shirt off to enable defibrillator placement.
- **Record:** KSL (S-58) reports Harpole said that once Kirk was on a gurney in a hospital room he cut the Freedom T-shirt off to prep him for a defibrillator; getting on top of Kirk is not covered.
  - S-58 para 21 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "Then Harpole said he cut Kirk’s Freedom T-shirt off to prep him for a defibrillator."
  - S-58 para 21 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "When they got to Timpanogos Regional Hospital in Orem, the team carried Kirk inside, put him on a gurney and took him into a room."

### C-10321 (ep 121)
- **Claim:** Brian Harpole states that the car door behind him remained open during the hospital ride because Charlie's leg was hanging out, and that Rick Cutler physically held Brian in place to prevent him from falling out.
- **Record:** The Salt Lake Tribune (S-59) and KSL (S-58) report Harpole said the car doors could not be closed because Kirk was so tall, and that Rick Cutler held his shirt to keep him from falling out.
  - S-59 para 24 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "Harpole recalled that Kirk was so tall — he was 6-foot-5 — that when he was lying in the backseat, they couldn’t close the car doors."
  - S-58 para 19 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "another private security operator, Rick Cutler, held onto Harpole’s shirt to keep him from falling out of the van"

### C-10323 (ep 121)
- **Claim:** In a clip the host plays, Brian Harpole says Frank Turek was praying out loud during the ride; the host says that, according to Brian, Frank was simply praying in the backseat and asks why he could not lean over and close the door.
- **Record:** KSL (S-58) reports that in Harpole's account Turek prayed out loud in the van; the host's question about the door is commentary.
  - S-58 para 19 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "Meanwhile, Turek prayed out loud."

### C-10324 (ep 121)
- **Claim:** Brian Harpole states that Rick Cutler reached across and held onto him so he would not fall out of the moving SUV while he attended to Charlie.
- **Record:** KSL (S-58) and the Salt Lake Tribune (S-59) report Harpole said Rick Cutler held onto him to keep him from falling out of the vehicle while he gave Kirk first aid.
  - S-58 para 19 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "another private security operator, Rick Cutler, held onto Harpole’s shirt to keep him from falling out of the van"
  - S-59 para 26 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "Harpole remembers dangling partway out of the car as another security officer held onto him so Harpole could try to provide care."

### C-10326 (ep 121)
- **Claim:** Brian Harpole states he applied approximately 36 ft (12 yd) of dressing, four 4x4s, and two hemostatic 4x4s to Charlie's neck wound during the short ride.
- **Record:** KSL (S-58) reports Harpole said he packed 36 feet of gauze, four 4x4s and two hemostatic 4x4s into Kirk's wound.
  - S-58 para 19 (tier2_attributed, Brian Harpole): "Harpole said he packed 36 feet of gauze into Kirk, along with four 4×4s and two hemostatic 4×4s, and maintained constant pressure the entire time."

### C-10462 (ep 122)
- **Claim:** A podcast audio clip of Frank Turek, played during the episode, contains his statement that he did not believe there was an exit wound on the back of Charlie Kirk's neck during the car transport.
- **Record:** The bindover ruling (S-7, 07:46:29) finds the bullet did not exit Kirk's body but came to rest in the tissues of his upper back.
  - S-7 (07:46:29) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "The bullet did not exit Mr. Kirk's body, but came to rest and tissues in his upper back left of the middle line."

### C-10465 (ep 122)
- **Claim:** The host says Frank Turek has now gone on multiple shows and claimed that he did do CPR.
- **Record:** Turek is reported (S-27) as saying the team performed CPR on the way to the hospital; the 'multiple shows' part is not covered.
  - S-27 para 167 (tier2_attributed, Frank Turek): "Turek and the security team jumped into the SUV that they used to travel to the event and sped to a hospital while performing CPR and praying."

### C-10615 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** The host says every piece of evidence the influencers push relies on Lance Twiggs, who she says was immediately granted immunity by the feds and was not allowed to be cross-examined at this hearing even though arguments were made that his testimony amounted to hearsay.
- **Record:** Agent Davis testified Twiggs was given use immunity (S-4 01:05:12) and Judge Graf referred to his immunity agreements with State and federal prosecutors (S-8 28:09); the 'every piece of evidence' framing is the host's characterisation.
  - S-4 (01:05:12) (testimony, SBI Agent Brian Davis (witness)): "Yes, he was."
  - S-8 (28:09) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "his immunity agreements with the State and federal prosecutors"

### C-10620 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** In a hearing clip the host plays, Lance Twiggs testifies that he would not say with 100% certainty because of camera quality, but that the person looks like Robinson in terms of the shoes, the sunglasses, the jeans and, though he had not specifically seen him in that hat, he was usually wearing a hat.
- **Record:** Twiggs's recorded statement (S-5 02:28:23) says he would not say with 100% certainty because of camera quality but it looks like Robinson in shoes and sunglasses, and he usually wore a hat and jeans.
  - S-5 (02:28:23) (exhibit, Lance Twiggs, Robinson's roommate/partner (recorded interview, State's Exhibit 16)): "I wouldn't say with 100% certainty just because of camera quality, but that looks like him in terms of the shoes he's wearing, the sunglasses."
  - S-5 (02:28:23) (exhibit, Lance Twiggs, Robinson's roommate/partner (recorded interview, State's Exhibit 16)): "I don't think I'd specifically seen him wearing that hat, but he was usually wearing a hat and then jeans."

### C-10622 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** The host says a witness confirmed seeing someone else driving a gray Challenger, a bald man, late at 12:47 a.m., captured on a Nest video as the car returned to campus.
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified (S-3, 02:06:53) he remembers the report recording that the Nobles told investigators the driver of the vehicle on their doorbell video was bald (the 12:47 time and 'someone else' inference are not established).
  - S-3 (02:06:53) (testimony, Agent Hull, former Utah State Bureau of Investigation agent (witness; Rev also renders Holt/Hall)): "I do remember that being in the report, yes."
  - S-3 (02:07:01) (testimony, Agent Hull, former Utah State Bureau of Investigation agent (witness; Rev also renders Holt/Hall)): "I did not."

### C-10623 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** The host says the woman who captured the Ring camera footage, Miss Noble, told police she saw a bald man driving the gray Challenger accompanied by three other people; in the hearing clip the host plays, the witness answers, "I believe they thought that was the case."
- **Record:** Agent Hull testified he remembered the Noble report saying the driver was bald (S-3 02:06:53) and answered 'Yes, I believe they thought that was the case' (S-3 02:07:08).
  - S-3 (02:06:53) (testimony, Agent Hull, former Utah State Bureau of Investigation agent (witness; Rev also renders Holt/Hall)): "I do remember that being in the report, yes."
  - S-3 (02:07:08) (testimony, Agent Hull, former Utah State Bureau of Investigation agent (witness; Rev also renders Holt/Hall)): "Yes, I believe they thought that was the case."

### C-10629 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** Per text messages between Tyler Robinson and Lance Twiggs that the host displays on-screen, the messages included lines such as "I am stuck in Orem for a little while longer yet," "if I'm able to grab my rifle unseen," and "Some hate just can't be negotiated out."
- **Record:** The texts read in court (S-5 02:42:39; S-7 06:06:16, Exhibit 16.1) include 'stuck in Orem for a little while longer yet' and 'Some hate can't be negotiated out', and the Information quotes 'If I am able to grab my rifle unseen' (S-1 p. 8).
  - S-5 (02:42:39) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): ""I'm still okay, my love, but I'm stuck in Orem for a little while longer yet. Shouldn't be long until I can come home, but got to grab my rifle still."
  - S-1 p. 8 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "If I am able to grab my rifle unseen, I will have left no evidence."
  - S-7 (06:06:16) (exhibit, prosecutor Chad Grunander): "State's Exhibit 16.1, when the defendant texted Mr. Twiggs, "I had enough of his hatred. Some hate can't be negotiated out.""

### C-10630 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** The host says the text in which Robinson says he had hoped to keep the secret until he died of old age is odd because the official narrative is that he was suicidal.
- **Record:** Agent Davis read Robinson's text 'I had hoped to keep this secret till I died of old age' (S-5 02:42:39), also quoted in the Information (S-1 p. 8); the host's 'odd' remark is commentary.
  - S-5 (02:42:39) (exhibit, Agent Davis, SBI (witness)): "To be honest, I had hoped to keep this secret till I died of old age. I am sorry to involve you.""
  - S-1 p. 8 (charging_allegation, Utah County Attorney's Office): "To be honest I had hoped to keep this secret till I died of old age. I am sorry to involve you."

### C-10636 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** The host says Lance Twiggs confirmed her report that Tyler Robinson was not political; in the hearing clip she plays, Twiggs testifies that Robinson talked about politics more than he did, usually things he heard on the radio on the drive to work, and that he had never heard Robinson talk about Charlie Kirk specifically.
- **Record:** Twiggs's recorded statement (S-5 02:30:23-02:31:12) says Robinson usually talked about things heard on the radio on the drive to work and that he had never heard him talk about Charlie Kirk specifically.
  - S-5 (02:30:23) (exhibit, Lance Twiggs, Robinson's roommate/partner (recorded interview, State's Exhibit 16)): "He'd usually talk about stuff he heard on the radio on his drive to work in their work car since it sounds like their whole crew went in the same car most of the time"
  - S-5 (02:30:54) (exhibit, Lance Twiggs, Robinson's roommate/partner (recorded interview, State's Exhibit 16)): "I personally had never heard him talk about Charlie Kirk before specifically."
  - S-5 (02:31:12) (exhibit, Lance Twiggs, Robinson's roommate/partner (recorded interview, State's Exhibit 16)): "No, not really. Usually, if he did talk about politics stuff, it was relating to Trump or current policies being issued or voted on"

### C-10638 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** ATF forensic biologist Caitlin Oliver testified that characterizing media reports as "Mr. Robinson's DNA is found on this item" would constitute a "source attribution" that is "not scientifically defensible."
- **Record:** Oliver's testimony read back at S-7 04:54:47: she answered 'Correct. Yes.' that the headline would be a source attribution and 'Correct' that it would not be scientifically defensible.
  - S-7 (04:54:47) (testimony, defense attorney Mr. Burt (Rev label 'Mr. Grunander')): ""Question: So from a scientific standpoint, you couldn't read the paragraph that sets out the likelihood ratio in your report, whatever the number, and then blast the headline in the media, 'Mr. Robinson's DNA is found on this item.' That's a source attribution, is it not?" "Answer: Correct. Yes.""
  - S-7 (04:54:47) (testimony, defense attorney Mr. Burt (Rev label 'Mr. Grunander')): ""And that would not be scientifically defensible, would it?" "Answer: Correct.""

### C-10639 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** Caitlin Oliver testified that DNA samples from the trigger and trigger guard were mixed samples with at least 4–7 contributors, with "major" and "minor" labels referring only to DNA quantity contributed, not to any activity-based attribution.
- **Record:** Oliver agreed the trigger/trigger guard sample was consistent with at least four contributors (S-6 02:19:19); the ATF report lists Robinson as major component with three unknown individuals (S-7 06:20:38).
  - S-6 (02:19:19) (testimony, Caitlin Oliver, ATF forensic biologist (witness)): "Correct. Correct."
  - S-7 (06:20:38) (exhibit, ATF report (State's Exhibit 30), read by prosecutor Chad Grunander): "The trigger, the actual trigger to the gun and the trigger guard of the rifle, the DNA profile is at least one trillion times more likely if it originated from Tyler Robinson as the major component and three unrelated unknown individuals"

### C-10642 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** The host says that Caitlin Oliver confirmed, in testimony the host plays, that Tyler Robinson's DNA sample was the most degraded of all contributors found on the firearm (swab 1.3 of trigger/trigger guard).
- **Record:** Oliver answered yes that the major profile in swab 1.3 (trigger and trigger guard) had the largest amount of degradation (S-6 02:19:32), and the ATF report names Robinson as that major component (S-7 06:20:38).
  - S-6 (02:19:32) (testimony, Caitlin Oliver, ATF forensic biologist (witness)): "There was... Yes."
  - S-6 (02:13:56) (testimony, Caitlin Oliver, ATF forensic biologist (witness)): "1.3 is the swabs of the trigger and trigger guard of the rifle."
  - S-7 (06:20:38) (exhibit, ATF report (State's Exhibit 30), read by prosecutor Chad Grunander): "The trigger, the actual trigger to the gun and the trigger guard of the rifle, the DNA profile is at least one trillion times more likely if it originated from Tyler Robinson as the major component and three unrelated unknown individuals"

### C-10643 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** In the audio played, ATF biologist Caitlin Oliver testifies that the trigger and trigger guard swabs were swabbed together, so she cannot say which part the DNA came from.
- **Record:** Oliver testified 'they were swabbed together, so I wouldn't be able to say where the DNA came from' (S-6 02:18:53).
  - S-6 (02:18:53) (testimony, Caitlin Oliver, ATF forensic biologist (witness)): "So they were swabbed together, so I wouldn't be able to say where the DNA came from."

### C-10644 (ep 124)
- **Claim:** The host says the media kept saying Robinson's DNA was on the trigger, while Oliver is admitting it could be the trigger guard, the cover for the trigger.
- **Record:** Oliver testified swab 1.3 covered the trigger and trigger guard together and she could not say which part the DNA came from (S-6 02:13:56, 02:18:53); the media part is not addressed.
  - S-6 (02:13:56) (testimony, Caitlin Oliver, ATF forensic biologist (witness)): "1.3 is the swabs of the trigger and trigger guard of the rifle."
  - S-6 (02:18:53) (testimony, Caitlin Oliver, ATF forensic biologist (witness)): "So they were swabbed together, so I wouldn't be able to say where the DNA came from."

- **Record:** Hull testified Exhibit 12.1 adds red ovals/circles to identify individuals and blurs faces in the parking-garage footage (S-3 01:00:40, 01:02:03; S-2 06:44:53); the rooftop zoom difference is not in the cited facts.

### C-10866 (ep 127)

  - S-7 (07:47:33) (ruling, Judge Tony Graf Jr.): "Officer Bagley found a red and black screwdriver approximately 10 to 15 feet west of the guardrail on the roof of the Losee Center."

### C-11459 (ep 133)
- **Claim:** The host says that in the footage Robinson's limp is gone once he reaches the rooftop and he is suddenly able to sprint.
